### [1] [Passive-Dynamic-Walking-Inspired Dynamics Guidance for Energy-Efficient Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35935)
*Hyeonjin Choi,Joongheon Kim,Daekyum Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受被动动态行走启发，通过在训练早期引入倾斜重力场并配合课程化奖励，引导类人机器人发现更经济的步态，然后移除全部PDW引导进行标称动力学优化，从而在无需参考轨迹/步态相位/接触时序的情况下降低机械运输成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习节能的类人行走需要发现机械上经济的步态协调，而不仅是降低执行器出力。现有强化学习仅通过努力相关奖励惩罚间接引导行走的逐步力学，效率提升有限。

Method: 提出受PDW启发的框架：早期训练中用倾斜重力场在平面碰撞几何上辅助矢状面前进，并配合课程耦合奖励项；在标称动力学优化前移除全部PDW特定引导。框架不需要参考轨迹、步态相位或接触时序。

Result: 在29自由度Unitree G1上五种子前向运动研究：0.5-2.0m/s指令速度下机械运输成本降低6.8-15.2%，且不损害速度跟踪。机械功分解显示降低源于正向执行器做功；奖励匹配对比区分了引导阶段更快的步态获取与倾斜带来的收敛经济性额外收益。框架可扩展到无辅助全向运动，在特定运动先验下速度匹配运输成本降低18.7%。硬件上前向运输成本在使用先验时下降16.3%，不使用先验时下降4.5%（后者在试验间波动范围内）。

Conclusion: 该PDW启发框架能有效发现更经济的类人步态，显著降低机械运输成本，并可扩展到全向运动与真实硬件，且无需参考轨迹、步态相位或接触时序。

Abstract: Learning energy-efficient humanoid locomotion requires discovering mechanically economical gait coordination, not merely reducing actuator effort. Reinforcement learning promotes efficiency through effort-related reward penalties, which guide the step-to-step mechanics of walking only indirectly. This article proposes a framework inspired by passive dynamic walking (PDW) that temporarily creates slope-equivalent conditions favorable to economical gait discovery and removes all PDW-specific guidance before nominal-dynamics optimization. During early training, a tilted-gravity field assists sagittal progression on flat collision geometry, complemented by curriculum-coupled reward terms. The core framework requires no reference trajectories, gait phases, or contact schedules. In a five-seed forward-locomotion study on a 29-DoF Unitree G1, the framework reduces mechanical cost of transport by 6.8-15.2% over commanded speeds of 0.5-2.0m/s without degrading velocity tracking. Mechanical-work decomposition attributes the reduction to positive actuator work, and reward-matched comparisons separate the guided regime's faster gait acquisition from the tilt's additional benefit to converged economy. The framework extends to unassisted omnidirectional locomotion, where its benefit persists once a walking-specific motion prior supplies kinematic coordination, the combination reducing speed-matched cost of transport by 18.7%. On hardware, forward cost of transport falls by 16.3% with the motion prior and by 4.5% without it, the latter within the trial-to-trial spread.

</details>


### [2] [In-Context Learning for Robots: Methods and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36012)
*Haojian Huang,Zexi Li,Junhao Guo,Yehang Zhang,Wenxuan Peng,Bohan Zhou,Weilin Ruan,Leyi Wu,Chenxu Wang,Jianchong Su,Binghui Xie,Wosong Chen,Yingjie Xu,Tianhao Zhou,Suzeyu Chen,Pukun Zhao,Jiaqi He,Xinyi Li,Runze Li,Peiran Dong,Shaoxiang Dang,Jing Huang,Yingbing Chen,Yifan Chang,Tianyi Zhang,Shiyuan Deng,Haozhi Wang,Yangkai Wei,Wenqian Li,Han Yang,Kaiwen Zhou,Huaping Liu,James Cheng,Rui Shao,Donglin Wang,Yaochu Jin,Jianye Hao,Ying-Cong Chen,Yinchuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于机器人上下文学习（ICL）的综述，围绕连接上下文证据与执行的"接口"提出四类方法家族，并比较其迁移假设及训练、对应、记忆的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人需推断新任务要求并将其转化为恰当物理动作，ICL通过演示与交互指导已有能力、部署时固定神经参数来支持这一过程，但该领域缺乏对上下文证据如何连接执行的系统性梳理。

Method: 以连接上下文证据与执行的"接口"为组织主线，区分四类方法家族：上下文条件策略、几何演示迁移、基于世界模型的控制、基于技能与智能体的执行；在操作与导航任务中分析这些机制在物体、环境和执行条件变化时如何保持教学要求。

Result: 比较四类接口澄清了各自的迁移假设，以及训练、对应、记忆在使上下文有用中的作用；分析将方法设计与评估实践相联系，区分对教学的响应性、物理迁移能力和从保留经验中获益。

Conclusion: 提出研究议程，将组合式任务获取与忠实迁移同物理递归自我改进相连接，即经验提升学习后续任务的能力。

Abstract: General-purpose robots must infer what a new task requires and translate that understanding into appropriate physical action. In-context learning (ICL) for robots supports this process by using demonstrations and interaction to direct existing competence with neural parameters held fixed during deployment. We organize this literature review around the interfaces connecting contextual evidence to execution, distinguishing four families: context-conditioned policies, geometric demonstration transfer, world-model-based control, and skill- and agent-based execution. Comparing these interfaces clarifies their transfer assumptions and the roles of training, correspondence, and memory in making context useful. Across manipulation and navigation, we examine how these mechanisms preserve taught requirements as objects, environments, and execution conditions change. This analysis links method design to evaluation practices that distinguish responsiveness to teaching, physical transfer, and benefits from retained experience. The resulting agenda connects compositional task acquisition and faithful transfer with physical recursive self-improvement, in which experience improves the ability to learn subsequent tasks.

</details>


### [3] [SAKI: Skill Assembly and Kinematic Imitation from Human Videos for Long-Horizon Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36031)
*Yijie Lu,James Zhao,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAKI框架，将人类视频中的操作技能迁移到长时程移动操作任务中，实现技能的复用、组装与闭环全身执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类视频学习操作技能潜力巨大，但将能力从桌面场景扩展到长时程移动操作，需要适应和组合跨场景、跨机器人构型的演示交互，这一挑战尚未解决。

Method: SAKI包含三个环节：准备可复用的以物体为中心的技能，保留任务关键交互同时允许迁移路径自适应；根据目标和给定任务依赖选择并排序技能，将物体角色绑定到当前场景，并在技能间传递场景估计与机器人构型；通过全身运动学模仿生成协调的底盘、手臂与夹爪运动，执行时用持久物体估计维持任务参照并利用视觉反馈更新剩余轨迹。

Result: 真实机器人实验表明技能可跨布局复用，并能将独立演示的交互组合为连续的移动任务（如整理、擦拭）；消融实验显示，在全身优化与视觉反馈固定不变的情况下，任务条件的参照准备显著提升长时程任务完成度。

Conclusion: SAKI证明了从人类视频中提取的可复用技能能够组装并迁移到长时程移动操作任务，任务条件化的参照准备是提升长时程完成度的关键因素。

Abstract: Learning from human videos offers a promising route to acquiring diverse manipulation skills. Extending this capability beyond tabletop settings to long-horizon mobile manipulation requires adapting and composing demonstrated interactions across changing scenes and robot configurations. We present Skill Assembly and Kinematic Imitation (SAKI), a framework connecting human-video skill acquisition, cross-demonstration assembly and closed-loop whole-body execution. SAKI prepares reusable object-centric skills that preserve task-critical interactions while allowing transfer paths to adapt. Given a goal and supplied task dependencies, it selects and orders skills, binds their object roles to the current scene, and carries scene estimates and robot configuration between successive skills. Whole-body kinematic imitation generates coordinated base, arm and gripper motion. During execution, persistent object estimates maintain task references across viewpoint changes, while visual feedback updates remaining trajectories. Real-robot experiments demonstrate skill reuse across layouts and the composition of independently demonstrated interactions into continuous mobile tasks, including tidying and wiping. Ablation results show that task-conditioned reference preparation substantially improves long-horizon task completion with whole-body optimisation and visual feedback held fixed. Check https://aus.bot/research/saki/ for video demos!

</details>


### [4] [MagNav: A Dual-Core Magnetic Track Guidance Framework for Lighting-Invariant Navigation in Two-Wheeled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36091)
*Arpita Kumari,Anupam Chatterjee,Richa Srivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种采用五通道模拟霍尔传感器阵列与磁轨道引导的两轮倒立摆自平衡循迹机器人，通过级联PID控制（内环平衡、外环转向）实现不受光照干扰的稳定循迹，在超过10000 Lux强光下仍能准确跟踪，优于光学方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两轮倒立摆（TWIP）机器人循迹多采用红外传感器，易受阳光、阴影等光照变化影响，仅适合室内使用，因此需要一种不受光照干扰的可靠循迹方案。

Method: 采用五通道模拟霍尔效应传感器阵列感知磁轨道；控制上采用级联PID结构，内环利用IMU配合互补滤波器保持平衡，外环根据磁传感器读数调节转向；使用步进电机提供精确力矩控制，无需额外旋转编码器。同时加入光学传感器模块作对比。

Result: 测试表明，磁引导系统在超过10000 Lux的强光下仍能保持准确跟踪，而光学系统则精度下降甚至偶尔失效。

Conclusion: 该设计提供了一种可靠、不依赖光照的自主导航方案，适用于工厂、仓库和户外路径等场景。

Abstract: Two-Wheeled Inverted Pendulum (TWIP) robots are useful for studying how to control systems that are naturally unstable and have fewer actuators than degrees of freedom. Adding autonomous line-following to these robots is challenging because steering and balancing are closely linked. Most existing systems use infrared sensors, which can be affected by changes in lighting, such as sunlight or shadows, making them reliable only indoors. This paper presents a self-balancing robot that can follow a line using a magnetic track guidance system. By using a five-channel analog Hall-effect sensor array, the robot is not affected by optical interference. The control system uses a cascaded PID structure: the inner loop keeps the robot balanced using data from an inertial measurement unit with a complementary filter, while the outer loop adjusts steering based on the magnetic sensor readings. Stepper motors provide precise torque control without needing extra rotary encoders. For comparison, an optical sensor module was also included. Tests show that the magnetic guidance system keeps accurate tracking even in very bright lighting, over 10,000 Lux, while the optical system loses accuracy and sometimes fails. This design provides a reliable, lighting-independent solution for autonomous navigation in places like factories, warehouses, and outdoor paths.

</details>


### [5] [Scouting the Dynamics Gap: Test-Time Policy Adaptation via Action-Outcome Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36107)
*Yishu Li,Liyuan Geng,Xinyi Mao,Amber Li,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCOUT：一种动力学感知的元学习框架，通过共享信念隐空间持续修正对环境动力学的内部信念，实现操作策略的快速在线适应与稳健的仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略在受控环境中表现良好，但面对未观测的物理属性与动力学时需要部署时快速适应。现有测试时适应方法多依赖稀疏标量奖励，未能利用物理交互中丰富的几何与动力学反馈。

Method: 提出SCOUT，将动作预测策略与前向动力学模型通过共享信念隐空间耦合。元训练时内循环通过最小化动力学预测误差（对观测到的动作结果）更新共享信念隐变量，外循环优化网络以进行动作选择。部署时基于动作-结果不匹配更新隐信念，从而在线推断并适应未知物理动力学，且避免灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在仿真操作基准上显著加速在线适应，并在真实世界中实现稳健的仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: 通过共享信念隐空间耦合策略与动力学模型，可利用动作-结果误差实现无稀疏奖励依赖的快速在线适应，兼具仿真性能与真实世界迁移能力。

Abstract: While pretrained robotic policies exhibit impressive capabilities in controlled environments, unobserved physical properties and dynamics require these policies to rapidly adapt during deployment. Existing test-time adaptation methods typically rely on sparse scalar rewards, failing to exploit the rich geometric and dynamic feedback from the environment during physical interaction. To address this challenge, we propose SCOUT, a dynamics-aware meta-learning framework that enables manipulation policies to rapidly adapt by continuously revising their internal beliefs about environment dynamics. Our approach couples an action-prediction policy with a forward dynamics model via a shared belief latent space. During meta-training, an inner loop updates this shared belief latent by minimizing the dynamics prediction error against the observed action outcome, while the outer loop optimizes the network for action selection. At deployment, this structure allows the agent to infer and adapt to unknown physical dynamics on the fly. By updating its latent belief based on action-outcome mismatches, the policy automatically adapts without risking catastrophic forgetting. We demonstrate that SCOUT significantly accelerates online adaptation across simulated manipulation benchmarks and achieves robust sim-to-real transfer in the real world. Project webiste can be found here: https://liy1shu.github.io/SCOUT/

</details>


### [6] [KPI: A Promptable Kernel for Physical Interaction on Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36151)
*Yikai Wang,Honghao Zhu,Xiao Hu,Hao Zhang,Zelin Wang,Yip Fun Yeung,Ding Zhao,Lingfeng Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 KPI，一种可提示的物理交互内核，插入轨迹源与未修改的全身追踪器之间。轨迹源以"契约"形式指定每方向跟踪/顺应/力保持范围，内核依据跟踪误差与力估计在线调节手臂刚度、阻尼、参考与前馈。借助视觉-语言智能体，从单条指令生成轨迹与契约，实现无任务专用代码的绞盘操作、开门与搬运演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类人机器人全身追踪策略通用性强，但轨迹本身对接触交互信息承载有限，实际接触行为取决于执行控制器。单任务策略依赖轨迹与控制器联合优化，而通用系统通常假设预设或人工设定的控制器，缺乏任务无关的通用交互能力。

Method: KPI 作为轨迹源与未修改全身追踪器之间的可提示内核。轨迹源发送逐方向契约（跟踪、顺应或维持力范围），内核从跟踪误差与力/力矩估计中以接触频率自适应调整手臂的刚度、阻尼、参考轨迹与前馈项。并以智能体框架演示：视觉-语言智能体根据单条指令同时生成参考轨迹与契约。

Result: 在指令驱动的绞盘操作、开门、箱子搬运，以及脚本化的表面交互实验中验证了该方法。绞盘演示中，类人机器人可转动曲柄将第二台机器人完全吊离地面。

Conclusion: 通过把控制器从任务前固定改为由轨迹源下发的"契约"，KPI 使通用全身追踪器无需任务专用代码即可获得可提示的物理交互能力，展示了从语言指令到复杂接触任务（含承重吊升）的端到端可行性。

Abstract: Humanoids now walk, balance and reach with remarkable generality: one whole-body tracking policy follows references from a human, or from an end-to-end policy. That generality travels in the trajectory, and a trajectory alone carries limited information about the interaction it should produce: at contact, the executing controller determines how the robot behaves. Single-task policies usually reach hard interactions by optimising trajectory and controller together in simulation; general stacks usually assume a preset or hand-chosen controller. We present KPI, a promptable kernel for physical interaction between the trajectory source and an unmodified whole-body tracker. Instead of a controller fixed before the task, the trajectory source sends a contract: per direction, track, comply, or hold a force range. From tracking error and a wrench estimate, the kernel adapts the arms' stiffness, damping, reference and feedforward toward it at contact rate. We demonstrate KPI through an agentic framework: from one instruction, a vision-language agent writes both the reference trajectory and the contract, with no task-specific code. We demonstrate instruction-driven winch operation, door opening, and box transport, alongside scripted surface-interaction experiments. In the winch demonstration, the humanoid is able to turn a crank to hoist a second robot fully off the ground.

</details>


### [7] [SkillWeaver: Agentic Exploration over Neural Interaction Skills for Scalable Robot Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36171)
*He Zhu,Lusen Zhao,Kwan Man Cheng,Su Li,Katerina Fragkiadaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkillWeaver 是一个自主生成机器人经验的智能体框架，通过在“神经交互技能”（NIS）上进行探索，无需人工示教或固定脚本，即可大规模生成演示数据并蒸馏为视觉运动策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习依赖大规模演示数据，但遥操作采集成本高、难以扩展到多样化环境和长时任务；仿真虽可扩展，但现有数据生成流程多依赖开环控制器、预设技能序列或任务专用程序，缺乏自主性与闭环交互能力。

Method: 提出 SkillWeaver 智能体框架：以可复用、可参数化、闭环的“神经交互技能”（NIS）为基本单元，NIS 用强化学习策略实现闭环、接触丰富的操作；由 VLM 智能体推理下一步动作、调用并参数化 NIS、观察结果，并生成验证、反思与记忆来指导后续探索；探索过程组织为验证器引导的树搜索。

Result: 自主扩展到 14.1K 场景中的 39.1K 条演示，并蒸馏为视觉运动策略；在仿真基准和真实世界操作中，训练于 SkillWeaver 生成经验显著提升了对新物体、空间配置、任务和环境的泛化能力，并实现零样本/少样本的 sim-to-sim 与 sim-to-real 迁移。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于神经交互技能的智能体式探索是机器人数据生成的一种可扩展替代方案。

Abstract: Large-scale demonstrations have driven unprecedented progress in robot learning, yet collecting robot data through teleoperation is expensive and difficult to scale to diverse environments and long-horizon tasks. Simulation offers a scalable alternative, but existing data-generation pipelines often rely on open-loop controllers, scripted skill sequences, or task-specific programs. We introduce SkillWeaver, an agentic framework that autonomously generates robot experience by exploring over Neural Interaction Skills (NIS): reusable, parameterized, closed-loop policies that expose learned physical interaction capabilities to a reasoning agent. Given a task and a simulated environment, a VLM agent reasons about what to do next, invokes and parameterizes NIS to interact with the environment, observes their outcomes, and generates verification, reflection, and memory to guide subsequent exploration. We instantiate NIS as reinforcement-learned policies for closed-loop, contact-rich manipulation and organize exploration as verifier-guided tree search, enabling the agent to discover successful long-horizon behaviors without relying on predetermined execution pipelines. SkillWeaver scales autonomously to 39.1K demonstrations across 14.1K scenes, which we distill into visuomotor policies. Across simulation benchmarks and real-world manipulation, training on SkillWeaver-generated experience substantially improves generalization to novel objects, spatial configurations, tasks, and environments, and enables zero- and few-shot sim-to-sim and sim-to-real transfer. Our results suggest agentic exploration over neural interaction skills as a scalable alternative for robot data generation.

</details>


### [8] [Test-Time Adaptation of Manipulation Policies Under Actuator Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36182)
*Som Sagar,Ransalu Senanayake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TeAR方法，利用执行器遥测数据对冻结的机器人操作策略进行动作修正，提升硬件退化下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实硬件中电机发热、电流饱和、电压下降等会导致同一动作产生不同运动，而现有策略训练假设动作效果不变。遥测信号（关节温度、电机电流、供电电压）虽已采集，却仅用于日志或安全检查，未用于策略自适应。

Method: TeAR是一种与策略无关的方法，在动作到达底层控制器前对其进行修正，将冻结策略转变为遥测条件策略。它学习一个轻量级Transformer，将拟执行动作与实时执行器遥测结合，对动作各分量进行放大、衰减或偏置。

Result: 在涵盖8个策略族、5类操作任务的18个策略-任务组合上评估。在退化模型不匹配的配对评估中，TeAR成功率达31.8%，基线策略为25.6%，假设模型逆为30.6%。在实体机械臂上，TeAR在发热条件下无需在线微调即可提升10-15%成功率。

Conclusion: 利用板载遥测进行动作修正是提升操作策略在真实硬件退化条件下鲁棒性的有效且通用途径。

Abstract: Robot manipulation policies are usually trained under the assumption that a commanded action produces the same motion as it did during training even after hours of operation. Real hardware violates this assumption as the motors gradually heat up, current saturates near contact, voltage sags under load, thus the same policy action can produce a weaker, delayed, or noisier motion. These conditions are already measured by onboard telemetry, such as joint temperature, motor current, and supply voltage, yet this signal is typically used only for logging or safety checks rather than policy adaptation. We introduce Telemetry-Aware Action Rectification (TeAR), a policy-agnostic method that turns a frozen manipulation policy into a telemetry-conditioned policy by rectifying its outgoing action before it reaches the low-level controller. TeAR learns a lightweight Transformer that combines the proposed action with live actuator telemetry and amplifies, damps, or biases individual action components. We evaluate TeAR across 18 policy-task pairs spanning 8 policy families and 5 manipulation tasks. In an additional paired evaluation with degradation-model mismatch, TeAR achieves 31.8% success, compared with 25.6% for the base policy and 30.6% for an assumed-model inverse. On a physical arm, TeAR improves success under heating by 10-15% without on-robot fine-tuning.

</details>


### [9] [Design and Validation of an Antagonistic Tendon-Driven Dexterous Robotic Hand with Bidirectional Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36241)
*Chunghyeon Lee,Hyukjun Kwon,Sungeon Kim,Saehyun Moon,Seokhwan Jeong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仿人、人类尺度、轻量、3D打印的17自由度机械手，基于双向拮抗肌腱布线机制，可在掌侧与背侧双向抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧机械手多沿用人类手部形态，却继承了单侧抓取工作空间的局限，需借助腕部或手臂重新定向才能从对侧抓取。已有可反转手部多依赖非仿人、软体或任务专用手指布局，而传统五指仿人手仍主要为掌侧抓取设计。

Method: 构建人类尺度（长200 mm）、轻量（220 g）、3D打印、17自由度的仿人机械手，采用双向拮抗肌腱布线机制，除耦合关节外，各关节的屈伸及外展内收均由主动驱动完成，无需被动复位弹簧；该布线机制使所有自由度均可越过中位构型，在掌侧或背侧形成抓取闭合。

Result: 实验显示：电机到关节平均传动误差3.0%，平均关节传动带宽13.2 Hz，最大指尖力29 N，定位重复性达0.15 mm；Kapandji评分8，成功完成全部33种GRASP分类抓取类型，并实现掌侧与背侧抓取任务。

Conclusion: 在紧凑的人类尺度平台上验证了双向操作能力，表明双向拮抗肌腱布线机制可赋予仿人机械手双侧抓取能力。

Abstract: Dexterous robotic hands typically reproduce human hand morphology but inherit its one-sided grasping workspace, requiring wrist or arm reorientation to grasp from the opposite side. Existing reversible hands generally rely on non-anthropomorphic, soft, or task-specific finger arrangements, whereas conventional five-digit anthropomorphic hands remain designed primarily for palmar-side grasping. This paper presents an anthropomorphic, human-scale (200 mm length), lightweight (220 g), 3D-printed, 17-DoF robotic hand built on a bidirectional antagonistic tendon-routing mechanism, in which flexion/extension (except the coupled joint) and abduction/adduction at joints are actively driven without passive return springs. The proposed routing mechanism allows all degrees of freedom to cross their neutral configuration and form grasp closures on either the palmar or dorsal side. Experimental evaluation demonstrates an average motor-to-joint transmission error of 3.0%, an average joint transmission bandwidth of 13.2 Hz, a maximum fingertip force of 29 N, and a positioning repeatability up to 0.15 mm. The hand further achieves a Kapandji score of 8, successfully performs all 33 GRASP Taxonomy grasp types, and performs palmar- and dorsal-side grasping tasks, validating bidirectional operation in a compact, human-scale platform.

</details>


### [10] [Bilinear World Models: Learning Representations with Structured Dynamics for Efficient Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36305)
*Antonio Pariente,Ignacio Boero,Nikolai Matni,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种JEPA风格的世界模型，将隐动力学限制为双线性参数化，可结构性地保证动作可恢复性、防止表征崩塌，实现高效规划与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统世界模型学习任意隐动力学，规划与控制效率低，且易出现表征崩塌；作者希望用结构化动力学把建模负担转移给编码器，从而获得更丰富、可控制的表征。

Method: 在JEPA框架下，将隐动力学限定为双线性参数化形式；该结构可结构性强制动作可恢复性，从构造上避免表征崩塌。作者还论证一大类非线性动力系统存在某种变换使其动力学变为双线性。

Result: 在标准2D与3D控制任务上，可直接从高维观测中学得具双线性动力学的表征，规划时间减少近三个数量级，同时保持甚至提升控制精度；在更长时域规划与实时控制等更严苛设定下同样成功。

Conclusion: 双线性参数化的结构化世界模型能兼顾高效规划与准确控制，推动JEPA类世界模型超越短时域离线规划，迈向长时域与实时控制。

Abstract: World models jointly learn latent representations and dynamics that predict how high-dimensional observations evolve under actions. In this work, we propose a JEPA-style world model in which, rather than learning arbitrary latent dynamics, we restrict them to follow a bilinear parameterization. This structure enables efficient planning and control while shifting the modeling burden onto the encoder, encouraging richer representations that expose the controllable geometry of the system. In particular, this structured parameterization allows us to structurally enforce action recoverability, thereby preventing representation collapse by construction. Although prescribing a bilinear parametrization may appear restrictive, we show that a broad class of nonlinear dynamical systems admits a transformation under which the dynamics become bilinear. Empirically, we show across standard 2D and 3D control tasks that representations with bilinear-parameterized dynamics can be learned directly from high-dimensional observations, reducing planning time by nearly three orders of magnitude while retaining or even improving control accuracy. We also propose more demanding regimes of longer-horizon planning and real-time control, and demonstrate that our method succeeds in both, moving JEPA-style world models beyond short-horizon offline planning.

</details>


### [11] [ATLAS: Aligned Transport of Latent Structure for Reliable World Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36333)
*Ke Fang,Yupu Yao,Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出ATLAS训练目标，通过显式保留关系几何并校准全局潜分布，提升世界模型的规划可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型依赖表示几何进行规划，但仅正则化潜边缘分布无法确定用于动作选择的状态间关系；表示在转换为规划器使用的最终潜变量时，规划相关的新颖性结构可能被削弱。

Method: ATLAS将来自信息量丰富编码器表示的归一化成对结构迁移到规划潜变量，并使用Wasserstein嵌入匹配（WEMReg）通过一维Wasserstein-2传输校准其边缘分布；分析表明关系保留与边缘校准提供非冗余约束，并将有限候选规划稳定性与关系失真、潜尺度失配和预测误差联系起来。

Result: 在LeWM中实例化后，ATLAS在PushT、TwoRoom和OGBench-Cube上提升了低新颖性和高新颖性评估子集的目标达成平均成功率，其中高新颖性TwoRoom提升最大；表示与rollout诊断显示规划潜变量中新颖性相关结构更强、边缘校准更好、多步预测误差更低。

Conclusion: 保留规划相关的潜几何是世界模型可靠规划的重要要素。

Abstract: Latent world models rely on representation geometry for planning, yet regularizing the latent marginal alone does not determine the state-to-state relationships used for action selection. We show that this can cause planning-relevant novelty structure to be weakened as representations are transformed into the final latent used by the planner. We introduce Aligned Transport of Latent Structure (ATLAS), a training objective that explicitly preserves relational geometry while calibrating the global latent distribution. ATLAS transfers normalized pairwise structure from an informative encoder representation to the planning latent and uses Wasserstein embedding matching (WEMReg) to calibrate its marginal through one-dimensional Wasserstein-2 transport. Our analysis shows that relational preservation and marginal calibration impose non-redundant constraints, and connects finite-candidate planning stability to relational distortion, latent-scale mismatch, and prediction error. Instantiated in LeWM, ATLAS improves mean goal-reaching success across PushT, TwoRoom, and OGBench-Cube on both lower- and higher-novelty evaluation subsets, with the largest gain on higher-novelty TwoRoom episodes. Representation and rollout diagnostics further show stronger novelty-related structure in the planning latent, improved marginal calibration, and lower multi-step prediction error. Together, these results highlight preservation of planning-relevant latent geometry as an important ingredient for reliable world-model planning. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/atlas-world-model-72C4/.

</details>


### [12] [DORA: Divergence-Oriented Data-Relay Algorithm for Partially Connected Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36395)
*Jonathan Diller,Fernando Cladera,Camillo Jose Taylor,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究部分连接网络中异构无人机团队的通信调度问题，提出异构任务感知覆盖（HMAC）问题并设计DORA算法，以信息价值驱动通信，实验显示MRT解析延迟最多降低74.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机搜索与监测任务中，团队常处于部分连接网络，机器人需在探索环境与向队友中继信息之间权衡。当机器人能力异构时，对某一机器人无用的观测可能对具备互补探测能力的队友至关重要，而传统仅依据发现或时间进行通信调度的方法忽略了这种信息价值差异。

Method: 论文将问题形式化为异构任务感知覆盖（HMAC）问题，耦合区域全覆盖与间歇通信下能力受限的任务相关目标（MRT）发现。提出DORA算法，以面向分歧的数据中继方式驱动通信，通过量化机器人当前信息状态与对各队友知识估计之间的任务相关分歧，综合任务相关性、发现新颖性、传感器不确定性和信息年龄来决定通信行为。

Result: 在四种具有不同物体密度和空间结构的仿真环境中进行评估，并在实体无人机平台上验证。结果表明，相比传统基于时间的通信调度方法，DORA将MRT解析延迟最多改善74.8%。

Conclusion: 以信息对团队的价值而非单纯发现来驱动通信，可有效缓解异构无人机团队在间歇通信下的探索—中继权衡，显著加快任务相关目标的发现与确认。该策略在仿真与实体平台上均得到验证。

Abstract: Teams of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) deployed for search and monitoring missions frequently operate as partially connected networks, forcing each robot to trade off exploring the environment against relaying information to teammates. This tradeoff is especially acute when robots are semantically heterogeneous: an observation that appears uninformative to the robot that made it may be critical to a teammate with complementary detection capabilities. In this work, we formalize this setting as the heterogeneous mission-aware coverage (HMAC) problem, which couples complete multi-robot coverage of an area with capability-constrained mission-relevant target (MRT) discovery under intermittent communication. We then present DORA, a divergence-oriented data-relay algorithm that drives communication by the value of information to the team rather than by discovery alone. DORA quantifies the mission-relevant divergence between a robot's current information state and its estimate of each teammate's knowledge, capturing mission relevance, discovery novelty, sensor uncertainty, and the age of information. We evaluate DORA in simulation across four environments with differing object densities and spatial structure, and validate it on a physical UAV platform. Our results show that DORA improves MRT resolution delay by up to 74.8% over traditional time-based communication scheduling methods.

</details>


### [13] [One from Infinity: Actualizing Futures from Pretrained World Models into Robot Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36413)
*Bang Du,Yichen Xie,Shuqi Zhao,Yuxin Chen,Menglin Wu,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"actualization"任务，用仅60M参数的轻量actualizer在冻结视频世界模型上实现任务条件化的未来选择与动作读出，性能超越大规模WAM/VLA且可单卡训练、低延迟实时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将世界模型转为机器人策略的方法依赖大规模机器人数据和算力微调重型世界模型骨干，成本高昂。作者认为昂贵的部分已在世界模型预训练中完成，其表征空间已涵盖多样的潜在未来，因此只需选择完成任务的未来并读出实现它的动作即可。

Method: 形式化"actualization"任务：在冻结世界模型提供的先验之上学习任务条件化的选择与实现。实现为RoboActualizer，在冻结世界模型编码器之上仅60M参数，由两个轻量DiT专家组成，通过flow matching联合预测未来latents和动作，整体可在单张32GB GPU上训练。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 2.0等仿真基准及两个真实平台上的五个任务中取得优异性能，可训练参数比现有WAM和VLA少至多100倍，延迟低至39ms，支持实时控制。

Conclusion: 冻结世界模型表征空间已足够，只需轻量actualizer完成未来选择与动作读出，即可高效低成本地将世界模型转化为可执行机器人策略。

Abstract: A pretrained video world model admits many plausible futures for a scene, but a robot must realize the exact task-conditioned one. To turn world models into executable robot policies, existing methods fine-tune the heavy world model backbone using large-scale robot data and computational resources. Challenging this status quo, we argue that the expensive part has already been paid in the world model pretraining since the representation space of a video world model lays out the diverse potential futures. In this case, what remains is to select the future that accomplishes the task and to read out the actions that realize it. We formalize this task as actualization, which learns a task-conditioned selection and realization on top of a prior supplied by a frozen world model. This can be solved by a tiny actualizer model. We implement RoboActualizer with as few as 60M parameters on top of a frozen world model encoder. The actualizer is composed of two lightweight DiT experts that jointly predict future latents and actions by flow matching. The model can be trained entirely on a single GPU with 32 GB peak memory. With up to 100x fewer trainable parameters than existing WAMs and VLAs, RoboActualizer reaches great performance on simulation benchmarks including LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, RoboTwin 2.0 and five tasks on two real-world platforms, with a low latency of 39 ms that allows real-time control.

</details>


### [14] [FineART: Fine-grained Annotated Robotic Trajectory Dataset and Vision-Language-Action Model for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36416)
*Jade Choghari,Pepijn Kooijmans,Mansi Agarwal,Yusuf Umut Ciftci,Aseem Doriwala,Catherine Weaver,Mouli Sivapurapu,Kai Yang,Jackson Lee,Thomas Wolf,Pragna Mannam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出密集标注双臂操作数据集FineART及自预测子任务的VLA策略FineART-VLA，显著提升长程任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境机器人需执行长时程、多步双臂任务，但现有数据集多为单臂且每回合仅一条高层指令，双臂数据稀少且子任务标注不全，难以支撑此类任务学习。

Method: 构建FineART数据集（40,543回合、1,718小时、533,913个子任务、151项任务，密集标注）；提出FineART-VLA策略，使其预测自身下一步子任务并进行mid-training。

Result: 空间消歧任务成功率从32.0%升至100.0%；在未见长程任务上，人工子任务分步引导使成功率从16.0%升至76.0%；在新机器人上仅需基线十分之一数据即可微调，并能零样本泛化到全新任务。

Conclusion: 密集子任务标注与自预测子任务的mid-training能有效提升双臂长程操作能力与跨硬件、跨任务泛化性，数据集、权重与代码将开源。

Abstract: Robots operating in real-world environments must execute complex, multi-step bimanual tasks over long horizons rather than single, isolated actions. Current manipulation datasets struggle to support this capability: although single-arm datasets reach hundreds of thousands of trajectories, they typically provide only one high-level instruction per episode while the rare bimanual effort that does label subtasks annotates only a fraction of its hours. We present FineART, a densely annotated bimanual manipulation dataset of 40,543 episodes, 1,718 hours, and 533,913 subtasks across 151 tasks. We also introduce FineART-VLA, a vision-language-action policy that predicts its own next subtask, and show that mid-training it this way yields substantial gains. Specifically, success on a spatial disambiguation task increases from 32.0% to 100.0%, and step-by-step human subtask guidance lifts success on an unseen long-horizon task from 16.0% to 76.0%. Furthermore, after minimal fine-tuning on a new robot, the policy requires one-tenth the data of baselines without mid-training and generalizes zero-shot to completely unseen tasks on the new hardware. We open-source the full dataset, model weights, and training code.

</details>


### [15] [Losing the name before the box: measuring and repairing what narrow fine-tuning costs a detector outside its deployment vocabulary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36426)
*Trung Minh Bui,Jongsul Moon,YoungOuk Kim,Jung-Hoon Hwang,Dongin Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文揭示微调检测器会悄然丧失对词汇未命名物体的覆盖能力，并提出一种无需训练的状态混合修复方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测器在窄域微调后域内精度提升即被部署，但其对词汇从未命名的物体（在障碍检测与巡检中带来风险）的覆盖能力变化无人关注，且域内测试集不含此类样本，现有指标无法察觉。

Method: 提出一种纵向协议：以同一预训练检查点与其微调后代对比，追踪保留的 top-K 提议覆盖率 C_τ，即预训练覆盖但词汇遗漏的类别中，检测器 top-K 区域仍覆盖的框比例；并在四种架构、三个域上验证，另测试冻结梯度阶梯与状态混合修复。

Result: 域内精度上升时 C_τ 下降 5.12 至 63.35 个百分点（大于 1024 px² 的框）；域内指标与检测 AP 均无法识别该下降；适应损失 87% 的 AP 但仅损失五分之一覆盖；命名最先失效；三种无共同预训练的架构在丢失类别上一致，且模型从未学过的类别不丢失；混合四分之一预训练状态（含归一化统计）可提升所有单元格覆盖，域内精度损失至多 2.47 点，仅需一次额外评估。

Conclusion: 微调会系统性且结构化地牺牲对未命名物体的覆盖，这一代价被域内指标完全隐藏；将预训练状态部分混合回模型是一种免训练、低成本的修复手段。

Abstract: A detector pretrained on a broad corpus is fine-tuned on a narrow domain, its in-domain accuracy improves, and it ships. We ask what happens meanwhile to its coverage of objects the vocabulary never names, which in obstacle detection and inspection carry the risk. No in-domain test set holds an example of one. We give a longitudinal protocol: one pretrained checkpoint against its own fine-tuned descendants. It tracks held-out top-$K$ proposal coverage $C_τ$: of categories pretraining covered and the vocabulary omits, the share of boxes a detector's top $K$ regions still cover. The quantity is the open-world proposal literature's; the longitudinal reading is not. $C_τ$ falls while in-domain accuracy rises, on four architectures and three domains, by $5.12$ to $63.35$ points on boxes above $1024$ px$^2$. No in-domain number identifies the fall, and neither does detection average precision, which charges a missed and a misnamed box alike. On the one architecture scoring both, adaptation costs $87\%$ of the AP against a fifth of the coverage, and the naming goes first at all six depths of its freeze ladder, every run. What breaks is structured: three architectures sharing no pretraining run agree on which categories lose coverage, and those a model never learned do not lose any. A repair follows and needs no training: mixing a quarter of the pretrained state back, normalisation statistics included, raises coverage on every cell swept for at most $2.47$ points of in-domain accuracy. Seeing it costs one extra evaluation pass.

</details>


### [16] [World4Scorer: Outcome-Grounded World Modeling for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36438)
*Jieyuan Pei,Meiyi Lu,Sining Ang,Yubo Zhao,Zhangyi Hu,Mingwei Xu,Haokai Ding,Wei Li,Zihan You,Jianwei Zheng,Li Yu,Yifeng Pan,Ji Tao,Rongjunchen Zhang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: World4Scorer 将规划评分器构建为轨迹条件化的 JEPA 式预测器，用模拟器结果标签监督所有候选轨迹状态，并以真实执行轨迹锚定预测，在 NAVSIM-v2、Bench2Drive 与 OGBench-Cube 上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成-选择式规划器需对从未执行的候选轨迹打分，但驾驶日志只记录已执行轨迹的未来，导致备选轨迹的预测缺乏约束。模拟器可为每个候选轨迹标注结果，因此需要一个能利用模拟结果监督并对齐真实场景演化的评分器。

Method: 提出 World4Scorer，将评分器设计为轨迹条件化的 JEPA 式预测器：为每个候选轨迹预测一个状态并从中读取分数。模拟器结果标签监督所有候选状态，已执行轨迹的观测未来则锚定预测器以贴近真实演化。共享参数使锚定约束可作用于未执行计划的评分；训练时引入场景匹配的候选库补充低分计划，并采用惯性重排序保证相邻选择一致。未来信息仅在训练阶段需要。

Result: 在 NAVSIM-v2 上取得当前最优性能，在闭环 Bench2Drive 上获得较强的适配系统结果；在固定 LeWM 世界模型与规划预算下，基于结果的评分也提升了 OGBench-Cube 上的操作规划表现。

Conclusion: 结果表明，以模拟器结果监督并锚定真实演化的轨迹条件化评分器，能有效约束未执行计划的预测，兼具高性能与跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires choosing a safe and efficient plan as surrounding traffic evolves. Generate-and-select planners propose multiple trajectories and score them for execution, and they have outperformed representative direct-prediction baselines on NAVSIM. Their scorer must compare plans that were never executed. Driving logs record the future of only the executed trajectory, so matching the logged future can leave predictions for the alternatives unconstrained; a simulator, in contrast, can label the outcome of every candidate. We introduce World4Scorer, which builds the scorer as a trajectory-conditioned JEPA-style predictor: it predicts a state for each candidate and reads the candidate's scores from that state. Simulator outcome labels supervise the states of all candidates, and the observed future of the executed trajectory anchors the predictor to real scene evolution. Because one predictor produces every candidate's state, the anchor can constrain shared parameters used to score unexecuted plans, while the future itself is needed only during training. Generated candidates mostly score well, so a scene-matched bank adds low-scoring plans to the outcome supervision; framewise choices can conflict, so inertial re-ranking keeps consecutive selections consistent. World4Scorer achieves state-of-the-art NAVSIM-v2 performance and a strong adapted-system result on closed-loop Bench2Drive. With the LeWM world model and planning budget fixed, outcome-based scoring also improves manipulation planning on the OGBench-Cube benchmark.

</details>


### [17] [Staircase Policy: Streaming Inference for World-Action Models with Large Action Chunks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36471)
*Guoheng Sun,Chen Chen,Jin Wang,Ang Li,Teresa Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出STAIRCASE POLICY（S-WAM），将flow-matching VLA转为JEPA式世界-动作模型，通过流式推理与交错去噪实现长时程机器人操作，兼顾准确率与吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型（WAM）通过对未来观测的预测来条件化动作生成，可提升机器人操作性能，但未来预测给本就昂贵的迭代式动作生成带来额外推理开销。动作分块（action chunking）可摊薄该开销，然而在长执行时程中，后续动作仍以过时的观测为条件，导致性能下降。

Method: 提出STAIRCASE POLICY流式推理与训练框架：把flow-matching VLA改造为JEPA风格的WAM；将大动作块在交错（staggered）去噪阶段切分为多个子块；近期动作一就绪即执行，后续动作继续精化；在每个子块边界处，基于最新观测重新预测未来潜变量并更新所有未执行动作，从而无需重复完整策略推理即可支持长时程执行。未来预测误差还可作为自适应分块的信号。

Result: S-WAM在LIBERO上达97.7%，LIBERO-Plus上达87.9%，并在多种策略骨干与真实机器人任务上均有提升。吞吐达每秒292.7个已执行动作，在精度相当下为常规执行方式的3.62倍；首动作时延从123.6 ms降至73.3 ms。叠加额外推理优化后，吞吐进一步提升至每秒642.9个动作。

Conclusion: 通过流式交错去噪与未来潜变量重预测，S-WAM在不牺牲精度的前提下显著提升长时程操作性能与执行吞吐，并降低首动作延迟，具备跨骨干与真实场景的通用性。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) improve robotic manipulation by conditioning action generation on predicted future observations, but future prediction adds further inference overhead to already expensive iterative action generation. Action chunking can amortize this cost over multiple actions, yet performance degrades over long execution horizons because later actions remain conditioned on stale observations. We introduce STAIRCASE POLICY, a streaming inference and training framework that turns a flow-matching VLA into a JEPA-style WAM and partitions a large action chunk into sub-chunks at staggered denoising stages. Near-term actions are executed as soon as they become available, while later actions continue to be refined. At each sub-chunk boundary, the future latent is re-predicted from the latest observation and used to update all unexecuted actions, enabling long-horizon execution without repeated full policy inference. The resulting future-prediction error can further serve as a signal for adaptive chunking. S-WAM achieves 97.7% on LIBERO and 87.9% on LIBERO-Plus, and improves performance across multiple policy backbones and real-robot tasks. It reaches 292.7 executed actions per second, $3.62\times$ the throughput of conventional execution at comparable accuracy, while reducing time-to-first-action from 123.6 to 73.3 ms. With additional inference optimizations, throughput further increases to 642.9 actions per second.

</details>


### [18] [DQ-MPCC: Dual-Quaternion MPCC for Quadrotor Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36482)
*Bryan S. Guevara,Luis F. Recalde,Guanrui Li,Tiago Nascimento*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双四元数模型预测轮廓控制（DQ-MPCC），将位姿表示为单位双四元数并在流形切空间投影轮廓误差，实现四旋翼竞速中更精确的过门控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼竞速需要激进姿态与进度控制并穿过每个门，而传统MPCC在惯性系定义预测模型、在机体系定义姿态误差，坐标系不统一导致跟踪精度不足。

Method: 采用单位双四元数表示位姿，将轮廓误差投影到双四元数流形切空间并在期望机体系表达，使用与常规模型相同的刚体动力学，在机体系下以统一的位姿-旋量坐标建模；通过蒙特卡洛软件在环仿真与真实八门赛道（11×4.5×3.65 m）与基线MPCC对比，仿真与硬件使用相同增益，机载运行100 Hz。

Result: DQ-MPCC所有完成飞行的过门均在规定几何裕度内，基线则超出；基线最差门偏差中位数仿真到现实增长71.5%，DQ-MPCC下降10.1%。在所有仿真过门均在裕度内的配置中，DQ-MPCC最小圈时仿真降低6.7%、实飞降低10.5%。

Conclusion: 统一的位姿-旋量坐标与流形切空间误差投影能显著提升四旋翼竞速的过门精度与圈速，并具备良好的仿真到现实迁移性与机载实时性。

Abstract: Quadrotor racing demands aggressive attitude and progress control while passing through every gate, and conventional quadrotor MPCC formulations state the prediction model in inertial coordinates and the attitude error in the body frame. We present a Dual-Quaternion Model Predictive Contouring Control (DQ-MPCC) for quadrotor racing in which the pose is a unit dual quaternion and the contouring errors are projected onto the tangent space of the dual quaternion manifold, expressed in the desired body frame: the same rigid-body dynamics as the conventional model, in unified pose-twist coordinates in the body frame. We compare DQ-MPCC against a baseline MPCC through Monte Carlo software-in-the-loop simulations and real-world racing on an eight-gate circuit of 11x4.5x3.65 m. With the same gains in simulation and hardware, DQ-MPCC keeps every crossing of its completed flights within the prescribed geometric margin, whereas the baseline exceeds it, its median worst-gate offset growing by 71.5% sim-to-real against a 10.1% decrease for DQ-MPCC. Among the configurations that keep every simulated gate crossing within the geometric margin, DQ-MPCC also reduces the minimum lap time by 6.7%, and by 10.5% in the real-world flights, while running onboard at 100 Hz.

</details>


### [19] [Closed-Form Cartesian Forward Kinetostatics for Spatial Multi-Segment Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36495)
*Ke Wu,Fangju Yang,Xiaohui Zhang,Junda He,Guanjun Bao,Jingang Yi,Jian S. Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出空间多段腱驱动连续体机器人的力-位形闭式（求积）模型，逐段传播无需迭代平衡求解，精度高且较基线快千倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间腱驱动连续体机器人的正运动静力学通常需对每个驱动输入进行非线性平衡求解，计算代价大，缺乏高效的力到笛卡尔位形的显式映射。

Method: 以笛卡尔骨干中心线与累积材料扭转作为广义坐标，导出应变度量与腱几何；由变分平衡得到显式轴向与弯曲关系，并在容许的非螺旋纵向走线条件下证明零平衡材料扭转；解按段逐段传播，无需迭代平衡求解，同时保留轴向变形、空间变化的轴向与弯曲刚度、腱走线直径及分段腱参与。

Result: 与全应变几何变应变模型（GVS）对比，单段与三段机器人最大长度归一化末端位置偏差分别为8.91×10^-6与1.01×10^-5；平均评估时间1.52 μs与2.94 μs，相对基线加速约1864倍与3348倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的显式力-位形映射在保持高精度的同时大幅提升计算效率，适用于空间多段腱驱动连续体机器人的快速正运动静力学求解。

Abstract: Forward kinetostatics of spatial tendon-driven continuum robots typically requires a nonlinear equilibrium solve for each actuation input. This paper develops a force-to-Cartesian-configuration model with a closed-form solution in quadratures for spatial multi-segment robots under tendon actuation. The Cartesian backbone centerline and accumulated material twist serve as generalized coordinates, from which the strain measures and tendon geometry are derived. Variational equilibrium yields explicit axial and bending relations and establishes zero equilibrium material twist within the proposed model for admissible longitudinal non-helical routing. The solution is propagated segment by segment without an iterative equilibrium solve, while retaining axial deformation, spatially varying axial and bending stiffnesses and tendon-routing diameter, and segment-dependent tendon participation. Numerical comparisons with a full-strain geometric variable-strain model (GVS) yield maximum length-normalized tip-position discrepancies of 8.91 x 10^-6 and 1.01 x 10^-5 for the single- and three-segment robots, respectively. Mean evaluation times of 1.52 μs and 2.94 μs, with corresponding speedups of approximately 1864x and 3348x over the baseline, demonstrate the computational advantage of the explicit force-to-configuration mapping in the reported benchmark.

</details>


### [20] [LIBERO-MAX: Do Robot Policies Adapt When the World Changes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36518)
*Yunbei Zhang,Zijian Jin,Yuanzhe Liu,Janet Wang,Xilun Zhang,Yuyou Zhang,Zhenyu Zhang,Daoan Zhang,Shuaicheng Niu,Gen Li,Jianfei Yang,Jihun Hamm,Ismini Lourentzou,Weirui Ye,Bo Liu,Peter Stone,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 LIBERO-MAX，一个包含 8,000 个配对案例的基准，用于评测机器人在任务执行中途发生场景变化时的鲁棒性，覆盖几何、观测、外观、杂乱和路径等八类变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人执行任务时常需在目标移动、视角变化或出现障碍后继续任务，但已有观测和已执行动作基于旧场景。许多仿真鲁棒性基准在重置时固定外部条件，忽视了这一时间维度的挑战。

Method: LIBERO-MAX 构建 8,000 个配对案例：每对固定任务、初始状态、策略随机种子和事件前动作序列，仅对比是否发生任务中途事件，从而区分事件相关性能退化与原本就存在的失败。评测涵盖十四种当前 VLA、混合及世界-动作策略，并包含相机控制与查询频率变化的实验。

Result: 中途事件使成功率下降 11.0–25.7 个百分点。事件画像显示策略对几何和观测变化存在共性脆弱点，而不同策略家族的排名相互交错。相机控制实验表明鲁棒性既取决于变化条件下的能力，也取决于遇到变化时所处的轨迹；改变查询频率并不能消除性能差距。

Conclusion: 配对协议与时间维度诊断使 LIBERO-MAX 成为可复现的测试平台，用于诊断任务执行中途变化引发的失败，并衡量机器人策略在环境持续变化下保持有效性的进展。

Abstract: Robots must often continue a task after a target moves, the viewpoint shifts, or an obstacle appears, even though their earlier observations and committed actions reflect the previous scene. Many simulation robustness benchmarks fix external conditions at reset, leaving this temporal challenge underexamined. We introduce LIBERO-MAX, a benchmark of 8,000 paired cases spanning eight types of changes to geometry, observations, appearance, clutter, and paths. Each pair compares task execution with and without a mid-task event, holding the task, initial state, policy seed, and pre-event action sequence fixed. This controlled comparison distinguishes event-associated regressions from failures already present without the change. Across fourteen current VLA, hybrid, and world-action policies, events reduce success by 11.0-25.7 percentage points. Event profiles reveal shared vulnerabilities to geometry and observation changes, while policy-family rankings interleave. Camera controls show that robustness reflects both competence under the changed conditions and the trajectory from which they are encountered; varying query cadence does not eliminate the gap. Together, the paired protocol and temporal diagnostics establish LIBERO-MAX as a reproducible testbed for diagnosing failures under mid-execution changes and measuring progress toward robot policies that remain effective as the world changes.

</details>


### [21] [Distilling Privileged Control Barrier Functions into RGB-Only Safety Filters for Dynamic Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36520)
*Seungyeon Yoo,Gawon Lee,Seungwoo Jung,Inkyu Jang,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出教师-学生视觉蒸馏框架，将特权CBF教师的安全行为迁移到仅用RGB的学生滤波器，用于动态环境安全导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅RGB端到端视觉导航策略在真实动态环境中易发生碰撞，需要专门的安全层。现有视觉CBF方法依赖实时渲染或显式场景重建，且主要针对静态场景，难以在机载部署。

Method: 教师使用真实机器人与障碍物状态，在real-to-sim动态高斯泼溅环境中构建安全约束，并考虑障碍物速度不确定性；教师仅用学生RGB历史中可观测的障碍物构建约束以缩小信息差。通过动作增强让学生接触多样的安全/不安全名义动作。学生将短RGB历史、机器人速度和名义控制动作直接映射为安全动作。

Result: 实验表明，该方法优于视觉CBF基线，并在动态障碍物运动下提升了RGB导航策略的安全性。部署时学生仅需RGB观测和机器人速度，无需显式3D重建或在线渲染。

Conclusion: 该教师-学生蒸馏框架可实现仅RGB的轻量级安全滤波，适用于动态环境下的机载部署。

Abstract: RGB-only end-to-end visual navigation policies remain vulnerable to collisions in real-world dynamic environments, motivating a dedicated safety layer. Existing visual Control Barrier Function (CBF) approaches seek to provide safety from RGB observations, but often rely on real-time rendering or explicit scene reconstruction and are primarily designed for static scenes, limiting their practicality for onboard deployment. We propose a teacher-student visual distillation framework that transfers the safety behavior of a privileged CBF teacher to an RGB-only student filter for dynamic environments. The student maps a short RGB history, robot velocity, and a nominal control action directly to a safe action, while the teacher uses ground-truth robot and obstacle states in a real-to-sim dynamic Gaussian Splatting environment. To reduce the teacher-student information gap, the teacher constructs safety constraints only from obstacles observable within the student's RGB history. It also accounts for obstacle-velocity uncertainty to improve robustness to motion variations, while action augmentation exposes the student to diverse safe and unsafe nominal actions to better capture the safety boundary. At deployment, the student requires only RGB observations and robot velocity, without explicit 3D reconstruction or online rendering. Experiments show that the proposed method outperforms visual CBF baselines and improves the safety of RGB-based navigation policies under dynamic obstacle motion. Project page: https://syeon-yoo.github.io/distill-cbf-site/.

</details>


### [22] [Trajectory-Level Mode Guidance for Controllable Diffusion-Based Multi-Robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36530)
*Tianyou Yu,Shengze Cai,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种在干净轨迹空间中进行引导的扩散生成框架，通过随去噪步数递减的引导强度逐步融入轨迹先验，在保留多模态性的同时实现可控轨迹生成，并自然扩展到多机器人协同规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动规划通常存在多个可行解，多模态生成对灵活的多机器人协调尤为有价值。扩散模型天然能学习这类轨迹分布，但如何在不限制生成的前提下融入粗略、不完整的轨迹先验仍具挑战。这类先验指示的是解空间中一个理想区域而非单一解，因此需要一种能保持多模态性的条件生成方法。

Method: 在干净轨迹空间中进行引导，并以与时间步相关的引导强度逐步引入轨迹先验。在每个反向扩散步，重建的干净轨迹提供了一个统一空间，用于整合规划代价与部分轨迹先验：规划代价通过基于梯度的细化引入，部分先验则在对应噪声水平上以递减的引导强度逐步注入。早期阶段引导生成趋向先验，随后逐渐释放约束以保留扩散模型固有的多模态性。该框架通过加入机器人间碰撞代价自然扩展到多机器人规划。

Result: 在单机器人和多机器人规划任务上的实验表明，该方法可实现可控的轨迹合成、多样的可行解以及安全的多智能体协调。

Conclusion: 通过在干净轨迹空间中进行时间步相关的引导，方法在融入粗略/部分先验的同时保留了扩散模型的多模态性，并有效扩展到多机器人协同规划，兼具可控性、多样性与安全性。

Abstract: Motion planning often admits multiple feasible solutions, making multimodal generation valuable, particularly for flexible multi-robot coordination. Diffusion models naturally learn such trajectory distributions, yet incorporating coarse and partial trajectory priors without restricting generation remains challenging. Such priors indicate a desirable region of the solution space rather than a single solution, motivating conditioned generation that preserves multimodality. In this paper, we guide trajectory generation in the clean trajectory space and progressively incorporate trajectory priors with a timestep-dependent guidance strength. At each reverse diffusion step, the reconstructed clean trajectory provides a unified space for integrating planning costs and partial trajectory priors. Planning costs are incorporated through gradient-based refinement, while the partial prior is progressively injected at the corresponding noise levels with decreasing guidance strength. This guides generation toward the prior in early stages while gradually releasing the constraint to preserve the inherent multimodality of the diffusion model. The framework naturally extends to multi-robot planning by incorporating inter-robot collision costs. Experiments on single- and multi-robot planning tasks demonstrate controllable trajectory synthesis, diverse feasible solutions, and safe multi-agent coordination.

</details>


### [23] [Asymmetric Scout-Worker Reconnaissance for Route Validation in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36537)
*Kashif Khurshid Noori,Jaskrit Singh,Athanasios Ch. Kapoutsis,Jing Xiao,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究未知环境中的非对称侦察-工作机器人路径规划，提出共生侦察框架，让敏捷侦察机器人仅为工作机器人探索必要区域以识别可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侦察机器人体积小、机动性强，其可通行路径对较大的工作机器人可能不可行；现有探索与重规划方法通常假设单一可通行模型并为同一机器人寻求最优路径，未明确处理该非对称侦察问题。

Method: 提出共生侦察框架，利用侦察机器人更优的机动性，仅探索未知环境中识别连接有序目标的工作可行路径段所需的部分，并从侦察观测中推断工作机器人可行性。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中验证：能确认可行路线、用经验证的工作可行绕行修复受阻路段，并相较于基线探索与规划方法显著减少侦察机器人行程；真实室内部署中侦察机器人穿越狭窄走廊识别出工作可行路线。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决非对称侦察-工作探索问题，提升路径验证与修复能力，并显著降低侦察开销，具备现实可行性。

Abstract: This paper studies asymmetric scout-worker reconnaissance in unknown environments, where a small, agile autonomous scout explores routes for a larger worker robot that must visit an ordered sequence of goal locations. Because the scout has a smaller footprint and greater mobility, a scout-traversable route may be infeasible for the worker; worker feasibility must therefore be inferred from scout observations. This setting is not explicitly addressed by existing exploration and replanning methods, which typically assume a single traversability model and seek optimal paths for the same robot performing the exploration. We introduce a symbiotic scout-based framework that exploits the scout's superior mobility to explore only the portions of the unknown environment needed to identify worker-feasible path segments connecting the ordered goals. Evaluations in simulated and real-world settings demonstrate that the proposed approach validates feasible routes, repairs blocked segments with validated worker-feasible detours, and substantially reduces scout travel compared to baseline exploration and planning methods. A real-world indoor deployment further demonstrates the scout navigating narrow corridors to identify a worker-feasible route.

</details>


### [24] [Reactive Real-Time Flow Policies via Asynchronous Distribution Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36540)
*Moritz Zoellner,Reece O'Mahoney,Ioannis Havoutis,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究VLA异步执行产生的动作分布问题，提出递归流场蒸馏与Propose-Resolve两机制以恢复反应性，显著提升异步策略成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略（如VLA）推理延迟与实时控制需求冲突。异步执行通过在执行上一动作块时预测下一动作序列来避免停顿。但作者质疑：异步执行是否产生与原始VLA相同的动作分布？发现对非马尔可夫演示，异步执行会产生根本不同的动作分布，从而限制策略的反应性。

Method: 提出两种互补机制对齐异步与原始VLA的动作分布：1）递归流场蒸馏（Recursive Flow-Field Distillation），利用VLA的动作生成流训练异步策略，并对所学分布进行理论刻画；2）Propose-Resolve，异步准备多个动作序列，用最新观测基于VLA动作分布下似然的轻量近似进行选择。

Result: 理论上刻画了所学分布；实验表明异步策略能生成原始VLA几乎全部范围的动 作，而现有异步方法仅恢复其中一小部分。在LIBERO上达到与原始VLA相同的成功率，在RoboMimic上保留约80%成功率，比现有异步方法高约30个百分点。

Conclusion: 异步执行在非马尔可夫演示下会改变动作分布、限制反应性；通过对齐异步策略与原始VLA的动作分布（递归流场蒸馏+Propose-Resolve），可有效恢复反应性并大幅提升异步VLA控制性能。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies such as vision-language-action models (VLAs) have achieved remarkable generalization, but their inference delays can conflict with the demands of real-time control. Asynchronous execution avoids pauses between action chunks by predicting the next sequence of actions while the robot carries out the previous one. In this paper, we study whether asynchronous execution produces the same action distribution as the original VLA. We find that, for non-Markovian demonstrations, asynchronous execution can produce a fundamentally different action distribution, which can limit the policy's reactivity. In our method, we seek to restore this reactivity by aligning the asynchronously produced action distribution with that of the original VLA through two complementary mechanisms. First, Recursive Flow-Field Distillation trains the asynchronous policy using the VLA's action-generation flow. We characterize the learned distribution theoretically and show experimentally that our asynchronous policy can generate nearly the full range of actions the original VLA would produce, while existing asynchronous methods recover only a fraction of that range. Second, Propose-Resolve prepares multiple action sequences asynchronously and uses the latest observation to select among them based on a lightweight approximation of their likelihood under the VLA's action distribution. Our resulting method matches the original VLA's success on LIBERO and retains about 80% of its success on RoboMimic, about 30 percentage points more than existing asynchronous methods.

</details>


### [25] [Planning Oriented 3D Scene Completion via Coupled TUDF Occupancy Representation Learning from Partial Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36543)
*Tianyou Yu,Pengfei Zhao,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向路径规划的3D场景补全框架，联合预测TUDF连续几何与体素占据图，并采用双向耦合学习提升重建与规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人导航中部分可观测性问题突出，传感器覆盖有限与遮挡导致环境大面积未观测。现有场景补全方法多聚焦于改良建图或重建部分观测的3D结构，鲜有探究如何设计场景补全以服务于路径规划等下游任务。

Method: 以部分LiDAR观测为输入，联合预测补全后的截断无符号距离场（TUDF）连续几何表示与逐体素占据图，形成耦合几何表述；引入双向耦合学习机制：TUDF特征为占据重建提供稠密几何引导，占据特征为距离场估计提供结构约束；网络直接预测完整占据与TUDF表示，无需后处理即可将TUDF融入轨迹规划。

Result: 在未见环境上的大量实验表明，该方法在几何重建质量和下游规划性能上均持续取得提升。

Conclusion: 面向路径规划的场景补全优于纯占据建模，耦合TUDF与占据的双向表示可有效提升障碍边界与自由空间几何推理能力，并为轨迹规划提供无缝集成。

Abstract: Partial observability remains a fundamental challenge in robotic navigation, where limited sensor coverage and occlusions leave large portions of the environment unobserved. Existing scene completion methods primarily focus on improving incomplete mapping or reconstructing partially observed 3D structures, but rarely investigate how scene completion can be designed to benefit downstream tasks such as path planning. In this work, we propose a path-planning-oriented 3D scene completion framework that moves beyond pure occupancy modeling toward a coupled geometric formulation. Specifically, given partial LiDAR observations as input, the proposed framework jointly predicts completed Truncated Unsigned Distance Field (TUDF)-based continuous geometric representations and voxel-wise occupancy maps. This coupled representation allows the network to better reason about obstacle boundaries and free-space geometry. To fully exploit the synergy between the two representations, we introduce a bidirectionally coupled learning scheme, where TUDF features provide dense geometric guidance to improve occupancy reconstruction, while occupancy features in turn offer complementary structural constraints that refine distance-field estimation. Consequently, the proposed network directly predicts complete occupancy and TUDF representations, allowing seamless integration of TUDF into trajectory planning without post-processing. Extensive experiments on unseen environments demonstrate that the proposed method consistently improves both geometric reconstruction quality and downstream planning performance.

</details>


### [26] [Learning to Explore Hidden Kinematics for Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36553)
*Ruiyao Liu,Boshu Lei,Zhuoyang Pan,Kostas Daniilidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将动作选择"摊销"进训练的策略学习方法：用贝叶斯滤波维护关节信念，并将其渲染为逐点关节流场来条件化策略，通过强化学习奖励信息增益，实现主动感知式关节物体操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭视觉推断关节物体的运动学往往存在歧义，交互可以消解歧义。已有主动感知方法在每一步贪心地寻找最能锐化信念的单一动作，但这种贪心搜索无法扩展到多步规划，因为需要接触与惯性动力学的前向模型，而这些模型本身也是未知的。

Method: 将动作选择摊销到训练中：维护关于关节类型与参数的信念分布，由生成式先验初始化，并通过贝叶斯滤波根据观测到的部件运动进行更新。为让策略以该信念为条件，将其渲染为逐点关节流场（即当前后验对物体上每个点预测的运动）。该表示携带关节运动的归纳偏置。使用强化学习训练策略，奖励每次交互从后验中消除的熵，从而把有信息量的探索变成习得行为，而非每一步都进行搜索。

Result: 在 PartManip 基准的门与抽屉操作任务上超越此前方法；在 ArticuRiddle（外观暗示错误关节的新数据集）上达到 61.7% 成功率，而此前最佳方法为 44.4%。

Conclusion: 把信念表示为关节流场比潜变量编码或从观测跟踪的流场具有更好的泛化性；以熵减少为奖励的强化学习能有效习得主动感知策略，在存在视觉歧义的关节物体操作中表现更优。

Abstract: The kinematics of an articulated object is often ambiguous from vision alone. Interaction resolves the ambiguity, and active perception methods exploit this by searching for the single action that most sharpens a belief over the kinematic parameters at each step. Such greedy search cannot be extended over a horizon without forward models of the contact and inertial dynamics, which are themselves unknown. We instead amortize action selection into training. We maintain a belief distribution over joint type and parameters, initialized from a generative prior and updated by Bayesian filtering on the observed part motion. To condition the policy on this belief, we render it as a per-point articulation flow field, the motion that the current posterior predicts for every point on the object. Carrying the inductive bias of articulated motion, this representation generalizes better than a latent encoding of the belief or flow tracked from observation. We train the policy with reinforcement learning, rewarding the entropy that each interaction removes from the posterior, so that informative exploration becomes learned behavior rather than a search at every step. Our method outperforms previous approaches across door and drawer manipulation on the PartManip benchmark, and reaches 61.7% success on ArticuRiddle, a new dataset of objects whose appearance implies the wrong articulation, against 44.4% for the best previous method. Project Website: https://hiddenkinematics.github.io/

</details>


### [27] [A robust single-sensing-element tactile sensor for concurrent pressure and tackiness detection with real-time signal decoupling capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36558)
*Ying Yang,Mingwei Gu,Jia-Sen Xie,Xingyu Ma,Yan-Na Lu,Lin Zheng,Jinhui Gu,Junshuai Chen,Yunjie Lu,Denys Makarov,Jin Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种表面柔软触觉传感器，将霍尔效应传感器与软磁复合材料集成于坚固弹性框架中，通过压入与拉脱时表面凸起动态改变霍尔-磁体距离，实现压力与拉脱力的双模态实时区分，具备高精度、高灵敏度与优异基线稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为人形机器人人工皮肤引入粘性感知可显著提升其认知与操作能力，但现有触觉传感器在多模态信号解耦和稳定性方面存在挑战。

Method: 采用霍尔效应传感器与软磁复合材料嵌入坚固弹性框架，构成单一传感元件的表面柔软触觉传感器；表面受压时内陷、从粘性表面脱离时明显凸起，从而动态改变霍尔传感器与磁体间的距离。

Result: 产生全过程可追踪且基线分离的信号，实现压力与拉脱力的实时区分；单传感元件在同一接触点实现双模态感知，并消除应力交叉干扰，提高精度与灵敏度；坚固框架与磁-机传感机制带来极高可靠性和优异的信号基线稳定性。

Conclusion: 该触觉传感器在评估粘附性能、监测橡胶老化、精确操作轻质物体以及感知自然物体表面特性等机器人能力提升方面具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Integrating tackiness sensation into the artificial skin of humanoid robots significantly enhances their cognitive and operational capabilities. However existing tactile sensors face challenges in decoupling of the multimodal signal and stability. Here we present a surface-soft tactile sensor that incorporates a Hall effect sensor and a soft magnetic composite within a robust elastic framework. The sensor surface indents under pressure and bulges prominently when retracted from sticky surfaces dynamically altering the Hall sensor-magnet distance. This generates whole-process-traceable and baseline-separated signals enabling real-time differentiation between pressure and pull-off force. This single-sensing-element design facilitates bimodal sensing at the same contact spot while eliminate stress cross-talk enhancing both accuracy and sensitivity. The fusion of a robust framework and magneto-mechanical sensing mechanism equips the sensor with exceptional reliability and excellent signal baseline stability. This tactile sensor holds substantial potential for advancing robotic capabilities in evaluating adhesive properties monitoring rubber aging precisely handling lightweight objects and cognizing natural objects surface characteristics.

</details>


### [28] [Riemannian Splat Regression Models for Learning Time Fields on Arbitrary Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36561)
*Anastasios Manganaris,Rohit Banerjee,Gokul Prabhakaran,Ahmed H. Qureshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需坐标投影、可直接在黎曼流形上学习任意函数的模型，将splat回归与缠绕高斯分布的splats结合，用于学习到达时间场并实现流形上的运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意黎曼流形上的运动规划困难，传统欧氏空间规划方法难以适用。现有基于神经网络逼近最优时间代价函数（如NTFields）的方法需要借助临时的坐标投影到更高维空间，否则无法直接应用。作者希望无需此类投影，以最低运行时间、最少模型容量在高维流形上获得最优规划。

Method: 将splat回归模型与由缠绕高斯分布（wrapped Gaussian distributions）定义的splats相结合，构建一个无需坐标投影即可在黎曼流形上学习任意函数的模型，并用其学习多个黎曼流形上的到达时间场。

Result: 成功在多个黎曼流形上学习了到达时间场，并将该方法的精度和模型规模与针对每个流形单独适配的多层感知机（MLP）进行了比较。

Conclusion: 所提方法无需坐标投影即可在黎曼流形上有效学习到达时间场，为高维流形上的最优运动规划提供了一种低运行时间、低模型容量的可行方案。

Abstract: Motion planning on arbitrary Riemannian manifolds is an important and difficult problem that frustrates typical planning methods for Euclidean spaces. In particular, motion planning methods that approximate optimal time-to-go functions with neural networks, e.g., Neural Time Fields (NTFields), cannot be directly applied without using ad-hoc coordinate projections into higher dimensions. Using these methods directly without such projections is desirable, as it promises to provide the lowest-possible-runtime method for obtaining optimal plans on high-dimensional manifolds while using minimal model capacity. In this work, we develop a model that requires no coordinate projection and can learn arbitrary functions on Riemannian manifolds by combining splat regression models with splats defined by wrapped Gaussian distributions. We successfully apply this model for learning arrival time fields on several Riemannian manifolds, and we compare the accuracy and model size of this approach with multi-layer perceptrons adapted to work on each manifold individually.

</details>


### [29] [EquivDP3: A SIM(3)-Invariant Point-Cloud Encoder for Data-Efficient Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36575)
*Abu Hanif Muhammad Syarubany,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将SIM(3)等变VNN编码器嵌入层次化扩散策略，在少样本人形全身操作中显著提升数据效率，代价仅0.8ms延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形loco-manipulation的视觉运动策略需仅凭少量示范泛化到不同物体位姿与光照。DP3用点云条件扩散动作生成，但其PointNet式编码器缺乏对操作任务固有的旋转、平移、缩放（SIM(3)）等变性。EquiBot已在轮式机械臂上用VNN编码器解决该问题，本文将其推广到更复杂的43自由度Unitree G1人形本体。

Method: 提出EquivDP3两阶段策略：高层扩散规划器带SIM(3)等变VNN编码器，输出6Hz全身指令块；由冻结的预训练RL运动策略以50Hz执行，手臂由微分逆运动学模块控制；端到端行为克隆训练。在IsaacLab两个仿真基准、4个非等变基线、5-100个示范、分布内外条件下评测，并做仅本体感觉的对照实验。

Result: 低数据区优势明显：5-10示范时67.1%成功率，基线38.2-52.4%；50-100示范时所有编码器收敛（74.3-85.2%），排序失去意义。仅本体感觉对照显示差距确为感知性：5示范去点云后31% vs 60%（EquivDP3），10示范78% vs 99%，50-100时差距消失，说明高数据平台是基准上限而非编码器学会了相同不变性。编码器每动作块仅增加0.8ms延迟。

Conclusion: 在层次化扩散策略的感知主干中注入几何对称性，是提升人形loco-manipulation少样本数据效率的实用且近乎零成本的方法。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies for humanoid loco-manipulation must generalize across object poses and lighting from only a handful of demonstrations. 3D Diffusion Policy (DP3) conditions a diffusion-based action generator on point-cloud features, but its PointNet-style encoder has no built-in equivariance to the rotations, translations, and scalings (SIM(3)) that manipulation tasks respect. EquiBot closed this gap for wheeled manipulators with a SIM(3)-equivariant Vector Neuron Network (VNN) encoder. We extend this to a substantially more complex embodiment, the 43-joint Unitree G1 humanoid, and propose EquivDP3: a two-stage policy where a high-level diffusion planner with a SIM(3)-equivariant VNN encoder emits 6 Hz whole-body command chunks, executed at 50 Hz by a frozen, pre-trained RL locomotion policy and a differential inverse-kinematics module for the arms, trained end-to-end by behavior cloning. Across two simulated IsaacLab benchmarks and four non-equivariant baselines (5-100 demonstrations, in- and out-of-distribution), EquivDP3's advantage concentrates in the low-data regime: at 5-10 demonstrations it reaches 67.1% success versus 38.2-52.4% for the baselines, while by 50-100 all encoders converge (74.3-85.2%) and the ordering is no longer meaningful. A proprioception-only control confirms this gap is genuinely perceptual: with the point cloud removed, success drops to 31% vs. 60% (EquivDP3) at 5 demonstrations and 78% vs. 99% at 10, but vanishes by 50-100, showing the high-data plateau reflects a benchmark ceiling, not five encoders learning the same invariance. The encoder costs only 0.8 ms of extra latency per action chunk over the PointNet encoder it replaces. Baking geometric symmetry into a hierarchical diffusion policy's perception backbone is a practical, nearly free way to improve data efficiency for humanoid loco-manipulation when demonstrations are scarce.

</details>


### [30] [Inferring Soil Friction Angle from Robot Foot-Ground Force Histories: A Bayesian Inverse Approach to Proprioceptive Soil Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36582)
*Dawei Xu,Zhijie Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文验证了四足机器人的足-地交互力信号能否用于识别无黏性土的内摩擦角φ。利用物质点法建立二维连续模型并以旋转腿力历程数据校准，训练两个高斯过程代理模型进行贝叶斯反演。在同模型实验中，14个非网格摩擦角的识别中位绝对误差约0.1°（最大约0.7°），可信区间均包含真值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人记录的足-地交互信号有望实现空间分布式的土壤强度原位表征，但尚不清楚能否从简化旋转腿的力历程中识别土壤内摩擦角。该研究旨在验证在正演模型正确设定时φ是否可识别，为基于本体感知的土壤传感奠定基础。

Method: 采用物质点法实现二维连续模型，并以实测旋转腿力历程作为基准进行校准；由该模型生成训练数据，训练两个高斯过程代理模型，对完整力历程进行贝叶斯反演，从而估计内摩擦角φ。

Result: 在同模型实验中，框架成功恢复14个非网格（离网格）摩擦角，中位绝对误差约0.1°，最大误差约0.7°；所报告的可信区间在所有情况下均包含真实值。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当正演模型被正确指定时，内摩擦角φ是可识别的。这支持进一步发展本体感知土壤传感以应对空间变化地形，应用涵盖机器人训练的物理基础世界模型以及野火后边坡评估等场景。

Abstract: Foot-ground interaction signals recorded by quadruped robots may enable spatially distributed, in situ characterization of soil strength. As a first step, we test whether the internal friction angle $φ$ of cohesionless soil can be identified from the force history of a simplified rotating leg. A two-dimensional continuum model implemented with the material point method, benchmarked against measured rotating-leg force histories, generates the training data, and two Gaussian-process surrogates support Bayesian inversion of the full histories. In matched-model experiments, the framework recovers 14 off-grid friction angles with a median absolute error of approximately $0.1^\circ$ (maximum $\sim 0.7^\circ$); the reported credible intervals contain the true value in every case. These results establish that $φ$ is identifiable when the forward model is correctly specified, and support further development of proprioceptive soil sensing for spatially variable terrain, with applications from physics-grounded world models for robot training to post-wildfire slope assessment.

</details>


### [31] [Cooperative Multi-Agent Vision-Language-Action Models via Reinforced Fine Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36588)
*Ruixiao Xu,Wong Lik Hang Kenny,Zhiqian Liu,Jianing Guo,Hanxiao Li,Kejian Shi,Shuning Zhang,Pu Feng,Yongjia Ma,Yuqing Ma,Kai Chen,Qi Dou,Yaodong Yang,Xianglong Liu,Simin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向多智能体视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的三阶段强化微调（RFT）流水线，解决预训练VLA缺乏细粒度协作能力的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在单智能体大规模数据上预训练，缺乏机器人间协作所需的细粒度协调能力。多机器人示范的监督微调（SFT）虽能部分弥补，但性能受限于示范数据，无法从自身经验中提升。

Method: 提出三阶段强化微调：1）初始化感知数据收集，扫描初始配置，仅在预训练VLA反复失败时引入人类示范，降低成本并增强对初始化偏移的鲁棒性；2）离线信用过滤微调，为各智能体分配信用，仅用正优势的个体轨迹而非联合轨迹微调；3）在线潜空间微调，冻结VLA在其潜噪声空间中进行RL，以应对在线RL在多智能体任务中因探索噪声和更新不稳定导致的低效问题。

Result: 在RoboTwin、RoboFactory及两台Franka机器人真实操作共11个任务上，以π0和π0.5为骨干评估多智能体VLA，平均成功率分别提升+23.1%、+16.4%和+44%。

Conclusion: 所提三阶段RFT流水线有效提升多智能体VLA的协作性能，并在仿真与真实环境中验证了其泛化性与有效性。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning (RL) methods for cooperative multi-agent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. This problem is challenging because VLAs are pretrained on large-scale single-agent data and therefore lack the fine-grained coordination skills required for inter-robot collaboration. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on multi-robot demonstrations partially bridges this gap, but its performance is bounded by the demonstration data and cannot improve from its own experience. We present a three-stage reinforced fine-tuning (RFT) pipeline for multi-agent VLAs. First, initialization-aware data collection sweeps over initial configurations and invokes human demonstrations only when the pretrained VLA repeatedly fails, yielding robustness to initialization shift with reduced human cost. Second, offline credit-filtered tuning assigns credit to individual agents and fine-tunes on per-agent trajectories with positive advantage rather than on entire joint rollouts. Third, we find existing online RL for VLAs are less effective for hard multi-agent tasks, which we attribute to noisy co-exploration and unstable updates. We instead use online latent-space fine tuning, which freeze the VLA and perform RL in its latent noise space. We evaluate our multi-agent VLA with both $π_0$ and $π_{0.5}$ backbones across 11 tasks in RoboTwin, RoboFactory and real-world manipulation with two Franka robots. Our multi-agent VLA improves the average success rate by $+23.1\%$, $+16.4\%$, and $+44\%$ on RoboTwin, RoboFactory, and real-world tasks, respectively. Code available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/mavla_rft-2BC0/.

</details>


### [32] [Simple Agentic Memory for Generalist Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36595)
*Yuyou Zhang,Yunbei Zhang,Miao Li,Janet Wang,Zijian Jin,Shilong Liu,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练记忆层SimpleARM，为冻结的通用机器人策略提供交互衍生的紧凑状态记忆，在RoboMME基准上16个任务平均成功率达67.17%，显著优于最强基线44.51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉记忆系统通常仅保留或压缩过去的观测，但机器人控制还需要无法由单帧显式表示的交互衍生状态，如持久身份关系、累积进度或有序流程。现有方法难以满足这类记忆需求。

Method: SimpleARM是一个免训练的记忆层，作用于冻结的通用机器人策略。它根据任务指令确定需要监控的内容；由冻结的感知工具在线维护紧凑的类型化状态；通过结构化访问仅在提出的子目标依赖历史时检索该状态；并通过当前视图对齐在执行前解析被回忆的实体。

Result: 在要求历史信息（当前观测已不可得）的RoboMME基准上，覆盖全部16个任务和三个策略种子，SimpleARM平均成功率为67.17%，而最强的非oracle基线为44.51%。匹配的消融实验显示机制特异性：移除关系、引用、进度或路径状态会在相应状态被检索用于控制的任务上造成大幅性能下降，而对其他任务基本无影响。

Conclusion: 结果支持机器人记忆的"基于状态"观点：有效的控制记忆并非简单保留的视觉历史，而是从交互历史中提炼的、紧凑且任务相关的状态。

Abstract: Visual-memory systems commonly retain or compress past observations. Robot control additionally requires interaction-derived state that no individual frame may explicitly represent, such as persistent identity relations, accumulated progress, or ordered procedures. We introduce Simple Agentic Robot Memory (SimpleARM), a training-free memory layer for frozen generalist robot policies. From the task instruction, SimpleARM specifies what to monitor; frozen perceptual tools maintain compact typed state online; structured access retrieves that state only when a proposed subgoal depends on history; and current-view grounding resolves recalled entities before execution. We evaluate SimpleARM on RoboMME, a benchmark of memory-dependent robot manipulation tasks that require history information no longer available in the current observation. Across all 16 tasks and three policy seeds, SimpleARM achieves 67.17% mean success, compared with 44.51% for the strongest non-oracle baseline. Matched ablations show mechanism specificity: removing relation, reference, progress, or route state produces large losses where the affected state is retrieved for control, while largely sparing other tasks. These results support a state-based view of robot memory: effective memory for control is not simply retained visual history, but compact task-relevant state derived from the interaction history.

</details>


### [33] [HACo: Learning Haptic Active Compliance for Force-Aware Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36596)
*Naisheng Ye,Yinzhe Zhou,Junkai Zhao,Yuhang Lu,Checheng Yu,Zhenjie Yang,Pengwei Wang,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HACo：一种基于触觉反馈的主动柔顺策略，直接从触觉反馈学习力调节动作，实现多接触力敏灵巧操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的灵巧操作需要将物理反馈转化为运动指令，并在多接触交互中调节接触载荷。现有策略常忽略指尖触觉与关节力矩反馈的互补性，且动作目标要么编码过大载荷，要么忽略受物体约束的运动。

Method: 提出HACo策略：通过柔顺调节遥操作将操作者输入转换为可执行的柔顺动作，保持运动意图同时调节载荷；以指令-状态差异作为辅助柔顺意图监督；融合局部指尖触觉与关节力矩反馈；设计柔顺接地模块，利用门控触觉交叉注意力将动作生成建立在演变的触觉状态上，实现闭环力调节而无需显式在线接触建模。

Result: 在真实世界基准上评估，涵盖多接触摩擦、切向交互、易碎曲面接触、旋转力矩和可变形物体操作；每任务20次试验中，HACo平均成功率达83%，最强基线为35%。

Conclusion: 结果证明HACo能在多样化的力敏灵巧操作任务中实现主动柔顺。

Abstract: Contact-rich dexterous manipulation requires policies that translate physical feedback into motion commands while regulating interaction loads across evolving multi-contact interactions. This requires haptic observations of contact state and action supervision showing how commands should adapt. Existing policies often overlook complementary fingertip tactile and joint-torque feedback, while common action targets either encode excessive loading or omit motion constrained by the object. We introduce HACo, a Haptic Active Compliance policy that learns force-regulating actions directly from haptic feedback. Compliance-regulated teleoperation converts operator inputs into controller-executable compliant actions that preserve motion intent while regulating loads. HACo learns these actions directly, using command-state discrepancy as auxiliary compliant-intent supervision. It combines local fingertip tactile responses with joint-torque feedback capturing load transmission through the articulated hand, including contacts beyond tactile coverage. A Compliance Grounding Module uses gated haptic cross-attention to ground action generation in the evolving haptic state, enabling closed-loop force regulation without explicit online contact modeling. We evaluate HACo on a real-world benchmark covering multi-contact friction, tangential interaction, fragile curved-surface contact, rotational torque, and deformable-object manipulation. Across 20 trials per task, HACo achieves an 83% mean success rate, compared with 35% for the strongest evaluated baseline. These results demonstrate active compliance across diverse force-sensitive dexterous manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [34] [Executor-aware Candidate Selection via a Feasibility Certificate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36597)
*Sooin Choi,Soonwoong Hwang,Wansoo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于证书的候选选择框架，用执行器硬约束构造命令见证并验证，在不改动候选生成/排序/执行器的情况下筛选几何可行但执行器不可行的候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化机器人系统中运动规划与下游执行器分离，几何上有效的候选可能无法被执行器的指令集实现，因此需要一种能判定候选是否可被执行的筛选机制。

Method: 从执行器硬集合在预测轨迹状态上构造命令见证，并对原始约束进行验证；保持候选生成、排序与执行器不变，另设事后精确线性规划（LP）准入基线作为对照。

Result: 在两种机器人模型共5,085次几何有效数值评估中，795次不存在执行器可行指令；证书充分但保守，795次均未被认证，而参考可行案例中有7.09%未被认证。FR3与固定基座RB-Y1仿真中证书准入常改变候选选择，LP基线显示保守性依赖平台；相比几何选择，证书准入与更低的规划器-命令覆盖率和更高的名义跟踪误差相关，在到达状态的交互余量上无一致优势。另用MoveIt/OMPL生成的外部候选池评估同一准入规则。

Conclusion: 证书准入可识别执行器不可行候选，但较为保守，且其代价与收益依平台而异，未必优于几何选择。

Abstract: Modular robotic systems often separate motion planning from a downstream executor that enforces state-dependent hard constraints. A candidate that is geometrically valid may therefore be incompatible with the executor's available command set. We present a certificate-based candidate-selection framework that constructs a command witness from the executor hard set at predicted rollout states and verifies it against the original constraints, without changing candidate generation, ranking, or the executor.
  Across 5,085 geometry-valid numerical evaluations on two robot models, 795 admitted no executor-feasible command. The certificate is sufficient but conservative: none of the 795 was certified, while 7.09\% of reference-feasible cases remained uncertified. In controlled FR3 and fixed-base RB-Y1 simulations, certificate admission frequently changed candidate selection, and a post-hoc exact linear-programming (LP) admission baseline revealed platform-dependent conservatism. Relative to geometry-based selection, certificate admission was associated with lower planner-command coverage and higher nominal tracking error, without a consistent advantage in reached-state interaction reserve. A planner-generated MoveIt/OMPL study further evaluates the same admission rule on externally generated candidate pools.

</details>


### [35] [OTRetarget: Joint Robot and Object Motion Retargeting via Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36602)
*Guillaume Besset,Erwann Carn,Timothée Carecchio,Valentin Tordjman-Levavasseur,Fabian Schramm,Yann de Mont-Marin,Justin Carpentier,Ajay Suresha Sathya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OTR ETARGET方法，用熵最优传输将人类示范中的多物体表面交互迁移到机器人形态，通过约束逆运动学联合优化机器人与物体姿态，实现无需缩放场景的运动重定向，并在OMOMO和实体G1人形机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将人类运动迁移到人形机器人需适应机器人形态，同时保持与环境的交互。对于移动操作任务，尽管身体比例不同，地面和被操作物体的接触必须保持一致。然而骨架运动本身无法完整描述这些交互，固定物体轨迹又会限制对新形体的适应。

Method: 将表面交互表示为有符号距离、最近表面点和相对方向，使用熵最优传输在人体、机器人和物体几何之间迁移这些量；将得到的交互目标纳入约束逆运动学公式，平衡接触保持与运动风格，并逐帧联合优化机器人与物体姿态，支持机器人-物体和物体-物体交互且无需缩放场景或示范。

Result: 在OMOMO上，机器人-物体交互Jaccard得分为87%，深度误差8.7 mm，而OmniRetarget为28%和29.3 mm；并在实体G1人形机器人上，用强化学习训练全身策略在重定向参考上完成双手箱子拾放至桌子等动作。

Conclusion: 该方法可统一联合重定向机器人与多物体运动，在保持交互一致性的同时适应不同形态，并成功迁移到实体人形机器人。

Abstract: Transferring human motion to humanoid robots requires adapting the demonstrated motion to the robot morphology while preserving interactions with the environment. This is particularly challenging for loco-manipulation tasks, where contacts with the ground and manipulated objects must remain consistent despite differences in body proportions. Yet, skeletal motion alone does not fully describe these interactions, and fixing object trajectories limits the adaptation to a new embodiment. In this paper, we introduce OTR ETARGET, a unified approach to jointly retarget robot and multi-object motion from human demonstrations. Our approach represents surface interactions through signed distances, closest surface points, and relative directions, and uses entropic optimal transport to transfer these quantities across human, robot, and object geometries. We incorporate the resulting interaction targets into a constrained inverse kinematics formulation that balances contact preservation with motion style and jointly optimizes robot and object poses at each frame. This formulation accommodates robot-object and object-object interactions without rescaling the scene or the demonstration. We validate the proposed approach on OMOMO, where it achieves a robot- object interaction Jaccard score of 87% and a depth error of 8.7 mm, compared with 28% and 29.3 mm for OmniRetarget. Finally, we demonstrate transfer to a physical G1 humanoid using whole-body policies trained with reinforcement learning on the retargeted references, across motions including two-handed box pick-and-place onto a table.

</details>


### [36] [RoboChrono: A Real Robot Benchmark for Streaming Task Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36605)
*Yuzhou Wu,Longteng Fan,Zimeng Li,Yu Wanchan,Ting Zhang,Yiyang Ma,Shihao Li,Wei Ying,Jianbin Qin,Jiajian Jing,Fangwen Chen,Yifan Wu,Zichen Zhang,Ruiqi Yang,Weibin Kong,Yihang Xu,Haoran Liu,Zonghang He,Xuyang Liu,YiFan Xiong,Siteng Huang,Tao Xu,Zhuo Xu,Long Chen,Ruoxiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboChrono，一个用于机器人操作流式任务理解的基准，包含 39 个场景、34,713 个评测实例，覆盖识别、对齐与时间定位三类共七项任务，揭示现有视觉语言模型在视觉匹配与时间排序能力上的显著不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解机器人操作过程需要模型结合交互历史与任务进度来解读视觉观测，但现有评测多依赖聚合分数，难以区分模型在视觉匹配、时间排序等不同能力上的差异，因此需要一个能力导向的诊断型基准。
