*Vincenzo Pomponi,Rocco Felici,Paolo Franceschi,Stefano Baraldo,Oliver Avram,Loris Roveda,Luca Maria Gambardella,Anna Valente*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DROM是一个语言引导的扩散框架，能够在单一生成策略中学习、表示并组合多种操作技能，用少量人类演示即可泛化并可靠完成长时程任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限的示范中学习面向多样、长时程任务的鲁棒操作策略，是机器人领域的根本性挑战。传统运动规划或硬编码控制器难以设计朝向敏感的行为，且演示数据采集成本高、泛化能力有限。

Method: DROM利用动态运动基元（DMPs）将少量专家演示扩充为表达力强的多技能数据集；在运动规划扩散（MPD）基础上，通过交叉注意力扩展扩散架构，支持语言条件下的多技能轨迹生成；对大语言模型对高层指令进行分解，生成可执行的技能序列，实现自然语言交互与自主执行。

Result: 在Franka Emika Panda、FANUC CRX25ia机器人以及MuJoCo仿真中，跨多种操作任务验证显示，DROM优于运动规划扩散和行为克隆基线，实现了鲁棒的多技能泛化，并能仅凭有限人类演示从自然语言指令组合技能、可靠执行长时程操作任务。

Conclusion: DROM表明语言引导的扩散生成策略结合DMP数据增强，可在少样本条件下实现多技能学习与长时程任务组合，具有良好的泛化性和实用价值。数据集、仿真环境等已在项目页面开源。

Abstract: Learning robust manipulation policies for diverse, long-horizon tasks from limited demonstrations remains a fundamental challenge in robotics. We present DROM, a language-guided diffusion framework that enables robots to learn, represent, and compose multiple manipulation skills within a single generative policy. DROM leverages Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) to augment a small set of expert demonstrations into expressive multi-skill datasets, substantially reducing data collection while improving spatial generalization beyond the demonstrated workspace. Building upon Motion Planning Diffusion (MPD), we extend the diffusion architecture to support language-conditioned multi-skill trajectory generation through cross-attention, allowing a single model to generate skill-consistent motions for a diverse set of manipulation primitives, including orientation-sensitive behaviors that are difficult to design using conventional motion planning or hard-coded controllers. For long-horizon manipulation, a large language model decomposes high-level operator requests into executable sequences of skills, enabling natural language interaction and autonomous task execution. We validate DROM on a Franka Emika Panda robot, a FANUC CRX25ia robot, and in MuJoCo simulation across a wide range of manipulation tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that DROM outperforms Motion Planning Diffusion and Behavior Cloning baselines, achieves robust multi-skill generalization, and composes learned skills to reliably execute long-horizon manipulation tasks from natural language instructions using only a limited number of human demonstrations. Datasets, simulation environments, and more at https://github.com/automation-robotics-machines/drom.

</details>


### [72] [Encore: Few-Shot Agentic Discovery of Manipulation Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37359)
*Yifan Kang,Zihan Wang,Zhiwen Fan,Bangya Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ENCORE，让编码智能体把少量演示当作"可读证据"而非训练数据，通过确定性构建器提炼多视角关键帧与夹爪事件等信息包，智能体据此编写策略程序，在 LIBERO-PRO 与真机上显著超越先前智能体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人任务通常仅以一句话描述，缺少抓取方式、接触顺序与目标形态等细节，智能体只能靠试错摸索；而少样本演示若仅用作训练数据，难以直接转化为可执行的策略程序，因此需要一种把演示转为可读证据的方式。

Method: 确定性构建器将每条演示蒸馏为多视角关键帧、夹爪事件、帧条与完整轨迹组成的信息包；编码智能体阅读该包，基于固定的感知与动作 API 编写策略程序，并在少量开发性 rollout 中迭代精化，随后冻结程序进入不暴露成功信号的密封评测。

Result: 在 LIBERO-PRO 上，智能体的首个程序在提供演示时已成功完成半数扰动任务，无演示时仅完成一个任务；冻结后的程序以 96.3% 超过同语言模型下最强先前智能体系统的 89.3%。在目标未言明的 RoboDojo 任务上，无演示时无程序成功。ENCORE 也运行于真实双臂机器人，各用五个演示学会方块交接与倒杯。

Conclusion: 把演示作为供编码智能体阅读的证据而非训练数据，可有效弥补语言指令的细节缺失，在仿真扰动任务与真实双臂操作上均取得优于先前智能体系统的表现，为机器人编程式策略生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Coding agents can now write, run, and debug programs with little human help. Robot tasks, however, are usually specified by a sentence that leaves out how to grasp, in what order to make contact, and what the result should look like, and an agent given only the sentence must find these details by trial and error. We introduce ENCORE, which gives the agent a few demonstrations as evidence to read rather than as training data. A deterministic builder distills each demonstration into a pack of multi-view keyframes, gripper events, frame strips, and the full trajectory. A coding agent studies the pack, writes a policy program against a fixed perception and action API, refines it iteratively over a few development rollouts, and freezes it before a sealed evaluation that never reveals the success signal. On LIBERO-PRO, the agent's first program already succeeds in half of the perturbed tasks with demonstrations and in one task without them, and the frozen programs outperform the strongest prior agentic system run with the same language model (96.3% against 89.3%). On RoboDojo tasks whose instructions leave the goal unstated, no program succeeds without demonstrations. ENCORE also runs on a real bimanual robot, learning cube handover and cup inversion from five demonstrations each.

</details>


### [73] [Towards Spatial Perception for Heterogeneous Robot Collaboration in Subterranean Mining Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37419)
*Mario Alberto Valdes Saucedo,Akash Patel,Christoforos Kanellakis,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PERSEPHONE自主采矿任务中的机载感知流水线，通过两个异构机器人（Explorer与Inspector）协作，实现废弃地下矿井深层矿床的自主探测与近距离检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一平台无法同时具备穿越退化巷道所需的机动性与表征矿体所需的传感能力，因此需要多智能体集成方案。

Method: Explorer机器人运行零样本视觉-语言语义分割栈，通过自然语言提示直接检测矿床，构建未知矿井的3D场景图；随后将地图与场景图传给携带高级传感载荷的Inspector机器人，用于规划近距离检测视角。几何抽象流程包括逐视图边界框生成、跨视图框合并、平面拟合与多边形提取。

Result: 在地下测试设施与活跃菱镁矿中进行了广泛的现场验证，覆盖铁脉与菱镁矿成矿，且在真实感知退化条件下完成测试。

Conclusion: 该机载感知流水线有效桥接了两个异构智能体，能够将原始检测结果转化为可执行的检测目标，验证了多智能体协作在自主采矿场景中的可行性。

Abstract: The autonomous extraction of deep mineral deposits in abandoned underground mines is fundamentally a multi-agent integration problem. No single platform simultaneously offers the mobility to traverse kilometers of degraded drifts and the sensing payload required to characterize an ore body. This article presents the onboard perception pipeline that bridges two heterogeneous agents within the PERSEPHONE autonomous mining mission. Which consist of a lightweight Explorer robot that maps an unknown mine and generates a 3D scene graph of inspection targets, by running a zero-shot, vision-language semantic segmentation stack that detects mineral deposits directly from natural-language prompts. The map and the graph are then handed to a second Inspector robot, which carries an advanced sensing payload and uses them to plan close-range inspection viewpoints. We detail the complete pipeline, with emphasis on the geometric abstraction that turns raw detections into actionable inspection targets, spanning per-view bounding-box generation, cross-view box merging, plane fitting, and polygon extraction, and we report an extensive field validation in a subterranean test facility and in an active magnesite mine, covering both iron-vein and magnesite mineralization under realistic, perceptually degraded conditions.

</details>


### [74] [FP2: Equipping Robotic Foundation Models with Force Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37433)
*Hongjie Fang,Shirun Tang,Junjian Hu,Shidong Zhang,Derek Zhang,Linhao Chen,Dehai Li,Mingyu Mei,Wanxi Liu,Cewu Lu,Shiquan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FP2是一个轻量级下游接口，为任务适配的机器人基础模型（RFM）赋予显式力控制能力，同时保留其动作生成能力，在四类真实接触丰富任务上一致提升性能与力调节质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型虽具备通用操作能力，但在接触丰富的场景中可靠物理交互仍是难题，需要显式力控制支持。

Method: 采用动作调控分解：任务适配RFM作为基础策略负责任务级动作生成，高频力控制策略专注交互调节；将基础策略上下文表征压缩后，与力/力矩及本体感受历史结合，预测结构化力控制参数。

Result: 在四个RFM骨干、四个真实接触丰富操作任务上，FP2一致优于对应基础策略，并与代表性力感知/力控制基线相比具有竞争力；消融显示基础策略上下文与物理反馈互补，保留基础策略动作生成提升了效率与新物体泛化。

Conclusion: 为RFM配备轻量力控制接口是提升接触丰富操作可靠性的有效途径，且不损害原有动作生成与泛化能力。

Abstract: Robotic foundation models (RFMs) are increasingly capable of general-purpose manipulation, yet reliable physical interaction remains challenging in contact-rich settings. We present FP2, a lightweight downstream interface that equips task-adapted RFMs with explicit force control while preserving their action-generation capability. FP2 adopts an action-regulation decomposition: the task-adapted RFM serves as a foundation policy responsible for task-level action generation, while a high-frequency force control policy focuses solely on interaction regulation. To condition force regulation on the ongoing manipulation, FP2 compresses foundation-policy contextual representations and combines them with wrench and proprioceptive histories to predict structured force-control parameters. We evaluate FP2 with four RFM backbones across four real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks. FP2 consistently improves task performance and force regulation quality over the corresponding foundation policies, while comparing favorably with representative force-aware and force-control baselines. Ablations further show that foundation-policy context and physical feedback are complementary for effective force regulation, while preserving foundation-policy action generation improves both efficiency and novel-object generalization. Project website: http://force-policy.github.io/fp2

</details>


### [75] [Anisotropic Representations Improve Planning in JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37441)
*Mingu Kang,Yoori Oh,Sookyung Kim,Joonseok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出潜世界模型中各向同性高斯正则导致的表示几何会影响规划代价，提出AnisoWM（含ΛReg）用可学习对角协方差替代固定各向同性目标，在不改变预测器与欧氏规划器的情况下提升四个视觉控制环境的规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型常在表示空间学习以动作为条件的动态，并用与目标表示的欧氏距离评估候选动作。联合训练中的正则虽可防止表示坍塌，但表示几何也决定了规划时终端误差的加权方式。作者发现：预测准确且表示未坍塌，并不保证潜规划代价与任务对齐；各向同性高斯正则会诱导出与任务代价排序不一致的几何，从而产生系统性错配。

Method: 提出AnisoWM及其ΛReg：将原先固定的各向同性高斯目标替换为可学习的对角协方差，并施加固定迹与各向异性约束。预测目标、预测器结构与欧氏规划器均保持不变，新的目标分布仅在训练阶段使用。理论上分析预测驱动下目标方差的分配方式、其对训练分布的依赖，以及所诱导度量在何种条件下能降低规划后悔。

Result: 在四个视觉控制环境中，AnisoWM的规划成功率均优于LeWorldModel；其潜规划代价与任务实际结果的吻合度也更好。

Conclusion: 表示几何是潜规划代价能否与任务目标对齐的关键因素，仅靠预测精度与防坍塌约束并不充分；通过在训练时将正则目标从固定各向同性改为受约束的可学习对角协方差，可在几乎不改变原有模型与规划流程的前提下改善规划性能，并提供了关于何时降低规划后悔的理论条件。

Abstract: Latent world models learn action-conditioned dynamics in representation space and often score candidate actions by Euclidean distance to a goal representation. Joint training typically regularizes the representation to prevent collapse, but the resulting representation geometry also determines how terminal errors are weighted during planning. We show that accurate prediction and noncollapsed representations do not guarantee a task-aligned latent planning cost: isotropic Gaussian regularization can induce a geometry that ranks feasible outcomes differently from the task cost. To address this mismatch, we introduce AnisoWM with $Λ$Reg, which replaces the fixed isotropic Gaussian target with a learnable diagonal covariance under fixed-trace and anisotropy constraints. The prediction objective, predictor architecture, and Euclidean planner remain unchanged; the target is used only during training. Our analysis characterizes the prediction-driven allocation of target variance, its dependence on the training distribution, and the conditions under which the induced metric reduces planning regret. Across four visual control environments, AnisoWM improves planning success over LeWorldModel in all four. Its latent planning cost also shows better agreement with task outcomes. Project website: https://rkdrn79.github.io/AnisoWM-page/

</details>


### [76] [Surgical Master Console Using General-Purpose Robot Arms and a Separable Articulated Distal Interface: Porcine In-Vivo Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37455)
*Minsung Kim,Seonho Shim,Dongho Yee,Younghoon Noh,Juahn Oh,Yechan Seo,Jinseok Lee,Jiyul Lee,Seong Jeong,Hyuk Choi,Youngbin Kong,Hyoun-Joong Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种腹腔镜手术主手：以通用机械臂为可编程基座，将手术专用交互集中在可分离的远端适配器上，双手机器人硬件总成本约5500美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能手术主控制台操作直观但昂贵且难以复现；现成商用触觉设备缺乏手术腕部关节与连续抓握的内置接口。

Method: 每条6自由度机械臂配备自研2自由度直驱腕、连续抓握传感及基于离合的工作空间管理；表征电流缩放、重力与启动摩擦，用于姿态相关重力补偿和运动门控辅助；统一主手状态驱动Isaac Sim或带RCM约束的双FR3平台。

Result: 主手状态发布到FR3目标更新延迟至多8 ms，末端跟踪延迟对齐残差均值0.31–0.66 mm；猪胆囊切除实验中5项操作者侧指标与手/末端速度分布，与120例临床Versius记录的99例胆囊切除人体在体遥测数据作描述性比较；主控制台未引发中断，术后7天观察无异常临床体征。

Conclusion: 结果表明可用通用机械臂构建手术主手，并将手术专用交互集中于可分离的远端接口。

Abstract: High-performance surgical master consoles offer intuitive articulated manipulation but are expensive and difficult to reproduce, while accessible commercial haptic devices lack built-in interfaces for surgical wrist articulation and continuous grasp. We present a laparoscopic master in which general-purpose robot manipulators provide the programmable base and surgical-specific interaction is concentrated in a separable distal adapter. Each 6-DoF arm carries a custom 2-DoF direct-drive wrist, continuous grasp sensing, and clutch-based workspace management; the complete bimanual hardware costs approximately US$5,500, below common bimanual commercial haptic references. To support sustained hand-guided use, we characterize current scaling, gravity, and break-away friction and use them for posture-dependent gravity compensation and motion-gated assistance, reducing continuous actuation and thermal loading. A common master state drives either Isaac Sim or an RCM-constrained dual-FR3 platform. Master-state publication to FR3 target update adds at most 8 ms, and tip tracking shows 0.31-0.66 mm mean delay-aligned residuals. In a porcine cholecystectomy experiment, five common operator-side metrics and hand/tip-speed distributions were compared descriptively with human in-vivo telemetry from 120 clinical Versius recordings of 99 cholecystectomy procedures; no interruption originated from the master console, and no abnormal clinical signs were recorded during the 7-day postoperative observation period. These results demonstrate a surgical master built from general-purpose robot arms with surgical-specific interaction concentrated in a separable distal interface.

</details>


### [77] [Contact-Adaptive Robotic Ultrasound Probe Control for Tissue Exploration and Continuous Task-Relevant Visualization Using Robot-Free Image-Motion Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37456)
*Seong Jeong,Minsung Kim,Dongho Yee,Juahn Oh,Yechan Seo,Jiyul Lee,Jinseok Lee,Seonho Shim,Younghoon Noh,Youngbin Kong,Hyoun-Joong Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"Sonologger"无机器人演示采集法与原位可解释控制器DIMC，实现随接触状态自适应调整的超声监测视图保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人超声多针对标准化切面、预设扫描协议或复现专家操作。本文瞄准不同角色：临床医生执行主操作时，机器人助手维持其选定的观察视图，使组织变化持续可见。

Method: Sonologger将六轴惯性传感器夹在医生手持探头上，无需机器人、相机或追踪器即可同步记录超声图像与探头相对旋转。不模仿轨迹，而是从记录中辨识医生纠正图像偏移所用的探头轴与方向；先补偿测得的图像延迟，再经"静止-移动-静止"分割得到相对旋转标签，平移因惯性漂移被排除在控制之外。这些量用于参数化DIMC（可解释、非学习型控制器），并配合束轴力调节与独立安全监督器（5 N强制回撤）。

Result: 在动态膀胱体模上，DIMC在7/7次盲测配对试验中获取并保持任务相关视图，静态保持仅1/7（McNemar p=0.031）；在注射器驱动的水扩张过程中保持视图，同时安全监督器限制了力超调。

Conclusion: 该框架为接触自适应术中监测提供了超声情境的可操作表征，临床验证仍待未来开展。

Abstract: Robotic ultrasound commonly targets standardized views, predefined scanning protocols, or expert-scan reproduction. We target a different role: while a clinician performs the primary procedure, a robotic assistant maintains a clinician-selected view so that changing tissue remains observable. We present contact-adaptive robotic ultrasound monitoring derived from robot-free demonstrations. Sonologger clamps a six-axis inertial sensor to the clinician-operated probe and records synchronized ultrasound and probe-frame relative rotations without a robot, camera, or tracker. Rather than imitating trajectories, the recordings measure which probe axis and sign the clinician uses to correct an image offset. A measured image latency is removed before still-move-still segmentation yields relative-rotation labels; translation is excluded from control because of inertial drift. These measurements parameterize the Demonstration-Informed Image-Motion Controller (DIMC), an interpretable, non-learned controller combined with beam-axis force regulation and an independent safety supervisor with 5 N forced retreat. On a dynamic bladder phantom, DIMC acquired and held a task-relevant view in 7 of 7 blind, pose-paired trials versus 1 of 7 for a static hold (McNemar p = 0.031), and retained the view during syringe-driven hydro-distension while the supervisor bounded the force excursion. The framework offers an operational representation of ultrasound context for contact-adaptive intraoperative monitoring; clinical validation remains future work.

</details>


### [78] [Waggle-Dance-Inspired Multi-UAV Recruitment with RGB and Synthetic Event Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37464)
*Zhang Nengbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于MuJoCo的仿真系统，将蜜蜂摇摆舞启发的运动通信机制用于空中机器人，对比RGB与合成事件视觉两条接收链路，验证从运动到完整消息、任务选择与执行的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜜蜂摇摆舞启发了"一个智能体的运动引导其他智能体后续工作"的通信机制。对于空中机器人，要实现这一机制需要打通"观测运动→完整消息→本地任务选择→执行"的链路，而RGB与事件视觉在该链路中的表现差异缺乏系统对比研究。

Method: 构建MuJoCo仿真系统。一个表演者向1至3个观察者广播6位任务报价，每个观察者独立解码两种报价后再选择任务。RGB接收端用稀疏光流提取轨迹；事件接收端仅依赖局部事件支撑与分组时序。两者共用同一运动字母表与显式空分隔符。由于事件静默不能确定目标可见性，事件版本加入物理捕获动作与连续性限定的空确认机制。

Result: 标称三观察者情形下，两种版本均实现12次完整消息接收、6个完成工作单元，以及在耗尽任务撤销后的重新分配。6组配对功能用例产生相同的消息与工作计数；额外的消息流丢失用例保持了受影响观察者的不活跃状态。回放42,060,633个记录合成事件复现了全部12个事件消息载荷与完成时间戳。标称任务总时间为RGB 493.80 s、事件517.40 s，差异主要来自事件捕获调度。

Conclusion: 这些固定仿真结果确立了两种接收端实现的功能可行性，而非传感器性能优势或硬件验证。

Abstract: The honeybee waggle dance motivates a communication mechanism in which one agent's motion guides the subsequent work of other agents. For aerial robots, realizing this mechanism requires a link from observed movement to a complete message, local task selection, and execution. This paper presents a MuJoCo system for comparing RGB and synthetic event vision within that link. One performer broadcasts six-bit task offers to one to three observers, each of which independently decodes both offers before selecting a task. The RGB receiver extracts trajectories using sparse optical flow; the event receiver uses only local event support and packet timing. Both use a common motion alphabet and explicit null delimiters. Because event silence does not establish target visibility, the event version includes a physical acquisition motion and continuity-qualified null confirmation. In the nominal three-observer case, each version produced 12 complete message receptions, six completed work units, and reassignment after withdrawal of a depleted task. Six paired functional cases produced the same message and work counts; an additional event-stream dropout case preserved inactivity of the affected observer. Replaying 42,060,633 recorded synthetic events reproduced all 12 event-message payloads and completion timestamps. Total nominal mission time was 493.80 s for RGB and 517.40 s for events, with the difference dominated by the event acquisition schedule. These fixed simulation results establish functional feasibility across two receiver implementations, rather than a sensor-performance advantage or hardware validation.

</details>


### [79] [Learning Social Navigation from Internet Videos in the Policy State Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37476)
*Jiaming Wang,Duc Thang Nguyen,Jizhuo Chen,Volodymyr Shcherbyna,Diwen Liu,Zhengcheng Shen,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种高效流程，将单目行走视频直接转换为闭环社交导航训练环境，仅用可通行性地图和行人轨迹表示场景，无需重建或渲染真实感观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练鲁棒的社交导航策略需要多样化的场景布局、地形和行人运动数据，但构建此类仿真环境并指定行人行为成本高昂。

Method: 基于"局部社交导航主要依赖两类信息：机器人可通行区域和附近行人运动"的观察，将静态场景表示为可通行性地图（可在反事实机器人运动下刚性变换），同时直接回放从视频恢复的行人轨迹；在策略状态空间中定义前向动力学，高效模拟反事实机器人状态。

Result: 在独立 Arena 基准上取得 81.2% 成功率，优于最强基线的 75.0%；在 20 次真机试验中成功 19 次，且无需策略微调。

Conclusion: 该方法以低成本将普通单目行走视频转化为闭环社交导航训练环境，所训练策略在仿真基准和真实机器人上均表现优异，具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Training robust social-navigation policies requires simulators with diverse scene layouts, terrain, and human motion, but constructing such environments and specifying pedestrian behavior is costly. We propose an efficient pipeline that converts ordinary monocular walking videos directly into closed-loop social-navigation training environments in the policy's state space. Our key observation is that local social navigation primarily depends on two types of information: where the robot can traverse and how nearby pedestrians move. We therefore represent the static scene as a metric traversability map, which can be rigidly transformed under counterfactual robot motion, while directly replaying the pedestrian trajectories recovered from the video over time. This abstraction allows us to define the forward dynamics directly in the policy's state space and efficiently simulate counterfactual robot states without reconstructing or rendering photorealistic observations. The resulting policy achieves 81.2% success in the independent Arena benchmark, compared with 75.0% for the strongest baseline, and succeeds in 19/20 real-robot trials without policy fine-tuning.

</details>


### [80] [Video2STL: Grounding VLM-Generated Temporal Specifications for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37519)
*Merve Atasever,Keyan Azbijari,Cagan Bakirci,Bo-Ruei Huang,Tolga Izdas,Zahra Shahrooei,Richard Yang,Erdem Biyik,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Video2STL 将纯观测视频转换为参数化信号时序逻辑（STL）规范，并以此形式化表示驱动机器人策略学习，实现跨本体迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视频的策略学习无需动作标注或本体匹配演示，但核心难题在于"从视频中应迁移什么信息"。现有方法将视觉观测压缩为标量相似度/价值信号，或让基础模型直接生成奖励代码，导致任务的时间结构难以被检查、落地和复用。

Method: 用视觉语言模型从视频中提取与本体无关的语义事件轨迹，构建符号化时序规范库；由模型决定任务结构，数值谓词阈值与时间边界则从成功机器人轨迹中落地。策略学习中分离短/长时标信息：短时程规范通过滚动窗口定量鲁棒性提供稠密奖励；长时程规范上的因果监控器为合法时序前缀提供一次性进度奖励。

Result: 四项操作任务中，Video2STL 平均 success-once 85.8%、success-at-end 67.0%，优于原生稠密 PPO（81.5%/59.5%）与 Text2Reward（65.0%/42.3%）；四足运动任务中，基于 Qwen 与 GPT 的 Video2STL 策略在 0.3–2.1 m/s 速度范围内均达 100% 成功率，并在高速能效上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 以 STL 作为视频到机器人的形式化中间表示为可解释、可落地、可复用的任务时间结构提供了新范式，并支持从人类或动物视频到机器人控制的跨本体迁移。

Abstract: Video-based policy learning is particularly promising, as it illustrates target behaviors without requiring action annotations or embodiment-matched demonstrations. A central challenge is deciding what information should be transferred from the video to the robot. Existing approaches commonly convert visual observations into scalar similarity or value signals, or ask foundation models to directly generate reward code. These approaches can make the temporal structure of a task difficult to inspect, ground, and reuse. We present Video2STL, a framework that converts observation-only videos into parametric Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications and uses the resulting formal representation for robot learning. A vision-language model extracts an embodiment-independent semantic event trace and constructs a bank of symbolic temporal specifications. The model determines the task structure, while numerical predicate thresholds and temporal bounds are grounded from successful robot trajectories. For policy learning, we separate short- and long-timescale temporal information: short-horizon specifications provide dense rewards through rolling-window quantitative robustness, while a causal monitor over a retained long-horizon specification provides one-time progress rewards for valid temporal prefixes. The same representation supports cross-embodiment transfer from human or animal videos to robot control. Across four manipulation tasks, Video2STL achieves $85.8\%$ average success-once and $67.0\%$ success-at-end, compared with $81.5\%/59.5\%$ for native dense PPO and $65.0\%/42.3\%$ for Text2Reward; in quadruped locomotion, Qwen-3.8 and GPT-5.6-based Video2STL policies achieve $100\%$ success across velocities from $0.3$ to $2.1\,\mathrm{m/s}$ while remaining competitive in high-speed energy efficiency. Project webpage: \href{https://video2stl.github.io/}{video2stl}.

</details>


### [81] [RawVLA: Embodied Neural Image Signal Processor For Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37530)
*Shuhong Liu,Heng Zhou,Lingfeng Qian,Yuhao Fang,Xianbao Hou,Qianyu Zhou,Lin Gu,Wei Sui,Jianfei Yang,Ziteng Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了ISP各维度对VLA模型的影响，提出自适应RAW处理模块RawVLA及评测基准RawVLA-Bench，显著提升退化成像下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常直接使用固定ISP生成的RGB图像，将成像流程排除在学习与评估之外，这一被忽视的设计选择对动作预测与操作成功的影响未被系统研究。

Method: 围绕增益、传感器噪声、色度响应、色调响应和位深五个ISP维度进行系统分析；提出RawVLA——一种流式神经ISP，为冻结的VLA策略自适应渲染RAW观测，并将容量集中于与具身行为相关的成像因素；同时构建RAW域操作基准RawVLA-Bench，覆盖干净与恶劣采集条件。

Result: 分析表明RAW到RGB的处理显著影响动作预测与操作成功率，且不同ISP维度影响差异明显；在RawVLA-Bench上，RawVLA在标准条件下保持性能，在成像退化条件下鲁棒性大幅提升。

Conclusion: 自适应RAW处理可作为物理相机与具身策略之间的有效接口，成像流程应作为显式的学习与评估变量。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models typically operate on RGB images produced by a fixed camera image signal processor (ISP), leaving the imaging pipeline outside the learning and evaluation loop. We systematically examine the consequences of this overlooked design choice across five fundamental ISP dimensions: gain, sensor noise, chromatic response, tonal response, and bit depth. Our analysis reveals that RAW-to-RGB processing materially shapes both action prediction and manipulation success, with different ISP dimensions exerting substantially different effects. Guided by these findings, we introduce RawVLA, a streaming neural ISP that adaptively renders RAW observations for frozen VLA policies while concentrating its capacity on the imaging factors relevant to embodied behavior. We further present RawVLA-Bench, a RAW-domain manipulation benchmark to expose image processing as an explicit evaluation variable across clean and adverse acquisition conditions. Experiments on RawVLA-Bench show that RawVLA preserves performance under standard conditions while substantially improving robustness under degraded imaging, establishing adaptive RAW processing as an effective interface between physical cameras and embodied policies.

</details>


### [82] [Wrench-ACT: Enhancing Robot Policies for Contact Rich Behavior Using Direct Wrench Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37552)
*Johannes Hechtl,Yannik Blei,Simon Ball,Reihaneh Mirjalili,Michael Krawez,Seongjin Bien,Philipp Schmitt,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模仿学习策略，将力旋量(wrench)作为唯一动作输出，直接由纯力控制器执行，用于接触丰富操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作需要主动调节交互力，但现有机器人操作学习多将动作表示为目标位置或位姿；引入力传感的方法要么仅将其作为观测，要么在预测力时依赖混合力控制器，未能充分发挥力域动作的价值。

Method: 以ACT（Action Chunking with Transformers）为基础架构，在双臂力反馈遥操作采集的力旋量示教数据上训练单任务策略，输出力旋量为唯一动作并交由纯力控制器执行；通过跨条件消融分析数据采集界面与力旋量动作空间各自的贡献。

Result: 在五个接触丰富操作任务上，力旋量策略全部达到或超过基于位置的基线，增益随任务所需的主动力调节程度而变化；消融实验表明双臂数据采集界面与力旋量动作空间各自独立地带来性能提升；将发布1000余条力旋量动作示教数据。

Conclusion: 力域模仿学习的关键在于数据采集，力反馈遥操作能捕捉操作者的主动力调节；以力旋量作为唯一动作输出配合纯力控制器，是接触丰富操作学习的一条有效路径。

Abstract: While contact-rich manipulation requires deliberate regulation of interaction forces, recent approaches to robot manipulation learning predominantly represent actions as target positions or poses. Even methods that incorporate force sensing either use it solely as an observation or, when predicting forces as part of the output, rely on a hybrid force controller. In this paper, we propose an imitation learning policy that predicts wrenches as its sole action output for direct use by a pure force controller. Our studies suggest that force-domain imitation learning depends critically on data collection, with force-feedback teleoperation improving policy performance by capturing the operator's deliberate force regulation. Using Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) as the base architecture, we train single-task models on bilateral wrench demonstrations and evaluate them on five contact-rich manipulation tasks. The wrench policy matches or outperforms position-based baselines across all tasks, with gains varying according to the degree of deliberate force regulation each task requires. Cross-condition ablations show that the bilateral data collection interface and the wrench action space each contribute independently to performance. To support further research, we will release over 1000 wrench-action demonstrations spanning these tasks on a companion website upon publication.

</details>


### [83] [Risk-Aware Semantic Grounding for Trustworthy LLM-Based Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37554)
*Łukasz Sobczak,Nur Keleşoğlu,Sławomir Piotr Nowak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种风险感知语义接地框架，将LLM机器人规划中的语义接地可靠性建模为多维风险估计问题，并引入TRUST-NAV基准进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用作机器人导航的高层规划器，但当指令存在歧义、环境不支持或语义不一致时，其输出可能不可靠，现有方法主要关注规划生成而忽视接地可靠性。

Method: 将语义接地可靠性形式化为多维风险估计问题，在规划前显式建模歧义风险、幻觉风险和语义冲突风险，使系统决定执行、请求澄清或拒绝指令。

Result: 引入包含标准导航任务和风险诱导指令场景的TRUST-NAV基准；实验表明传统LLM规划器在有效任务上表现强，而所提框架显著提升歧义检测和语义冲突拒绝能力。

Conclusion: 可信机器人规划的评价不应仅看任务完成度，还应考察系统识别何时不应执行的能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as high-level planners in robot navigation, but their outputs may become unreliable when instructions are ambiguous, unsupported by the environment, or semantically inconsistent. This paper presents a Risk-Aware Semantic Grounding framework for trustworthy LLM-based robot planning. Unlike existing LLM-based planners that primarily optimize plan generation, we formulate semantic grounding reliability as a multi-dimensional risk estimation problem. The proposed architecture explicitly models grounding uncertainty through ambiguity, hallucination and semantic-conflict risks before planning occurs, enabling the system to decide whether to execute the instruction, request clarification, or reject it. To evaluate the approach, we introduce TRUST-NAV, a benchmark containing both standard navigation tasks and risk-inducing instruction scenarios. Experimental results show that while conventional LLM planners achieve strong performance on valid navigation tasks, the proposed framework substantially improves ambiguity detection and semantic conflict rejection. These findings suggest that trustworthy robot planning should be evaluated not only by task completion, but also by the ability to recognize when execution should not occur.

</details>


### [84] [RoboFin3D: A Sim-to-Real Platform for Robotic Surface Finishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37560)
*Haowei Wen,Shangtao Li,Vaibhav Sanjay,Philip Huang,Jiaoyang Li,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboFin3D——基于 Isaac Sim 与 Newton 物理引擎的仿真到现实打磨/砂光平台，用 SDF 建模工件几何变化、用表面场建模外观渐进变化，并推出 WeldGen 生成焊缝样本；经真实实验校准，仿真数据可提升感知模型性能（SAM2 分割 IoU 从 77.15% 提升至 84.47%，合成+真实联合训练达 97.41%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人表面精加工（打磨、砂光）的实验依赖物理试件，成本高、消耗工件，导致实验难以复现，因此需要可重复、低成本的仿真替代方案。

Method: 构建于 Isaac Sim 与 Newton 物理引擎之上的仿真到现实平台 RoboFin3D；采用有符号距离场（SDF）表示工件不断变化的几何，无需中间网格即可完成接触计算、实时更新与渲染；另用独立的表面场建模砂光过程中表面外观的渐进变化；并提出焊缝采样模块 WeldGen，在 8,918 个真实工件网格上生成焊缝，以提供多样化仿真资产；仿真参数依据真实实验结果进行校准。

Result: 评估显示仿真与真实世界具有较好保真度；仿真生成数据可用于提升感知模型性能：仅用仿真数据微调 SAM2，未打磨区域分割 IoU 从 77.15% 提升到 84.47%；合成与真实数据联合训练则达到 97.41%。

Conclusion: RoboFin3D 为机器人表面精加工提供了低成本、可重复的物理仿真方案，验证了仿真到现实的迁移潜力，并表明合成数据能有效提升真实任务中的感知模型表现。

Abstract: Grinding and sanding are fundamental processes in industrial robotic surface finishing. However, physical trials are expensive and consume workpieces, making reproducible experiments difficult. We present RoboFin3D, a sim-to-real platform built on Isaac Sim and the Newton physics engine, that provides physics-based grinding and sanding simulation for cheap and repeatable robotic surface finishing experiments. RoboFin3D utilizes a signed distance field (SDF) to model the changing geometry of the workpiece, enabling contact computation, live updates and rendering without an intermediate mesh. It additionally uses a separate surface field to model progressive surface appearance change during sanding. We also introduce WeldGen, a weld sampling module, to generate weld beads on 8,918 real-world workpiece meshes for providing diverse simulation assets. The simulation parameters are calibrated on real experimental results and our evaluation demonstrates our simulation's fidelity against the real world. We also demonstrate that simulation-generated data can be used to improve the performance of perception models. Simulation-only fine-tuning of SAM2 improves IoU for segmentation of unsanded regions from 77.15% to 84.47%, while combined synthetic and real training reaches 97.41%.

</details>


### [85] [RoboHarn-Evo: Evolving Hierarchical Physical Knowledge for Self-Improving Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37583)
*Shifeng Bao,Fanding Huang,Yihan Lin,Youhe Feng,Guanlin Li,Chen Zhao,Yang Li,Jiawei He,Cheng Chi,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboHarn-Evo双环框架，通过物理交互经验演化分层物理知识（HPK），在不更新基座模型的情况下提升视觉-语言模型的机器人操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型能协调长程机器人操作，但任务推理的成功仍取决于局部物理交互能否产生预期效果；作者希望在不更新基座模型的前提下，利用重复交互来改进这一能力。

Method: RoboHarn-Evo是一种双环harness，从物理经验中演化分层物理知识HPK。HPK包含两层可复用知识：任务知识（判断该执行哪个子任务及其完成条件）与动作知识（物体相对几何策略及其物理效果）。执行时，智能体在对应决策层级检索知识并结合任务目标对齐当前场景；跨回合则利用物理反馈修正历史知识、更新适用性，并组织可复用条目供后续检索。

Result: 在RMBench上，HPK使不同智能体模型的平均成功率提升最高24.2个百分点。80次交互rollout后，留出集成功率：GPT-5.5从48.3%升至75.0%，GPT-6从70.0%升至88.3%。RoboHarn-Evo纠正了超过83%的历史知识错误，同时保留95.8%的有效知识，并从RMBench零样本迁移到RoboDojo，分别提升35.0和25.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 物理交互可以被积累为可复用知识，用于改进后续的机器人操作，验证了经验驱动知识演化在无需模型更新场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language models can coordinate long-horizon robot manipulation, yet successful task reasoning still depends on whether local physical interactions produce the intended effects. We study how repeated interaction can improve this capability without updating the base model. We introduce RoboHarn-Evo, a dual-loop harness that evolves Hierarchical Physical Knowledge (HPK) from physical experience. HPK couples two levels of reusable knowledge: Task Knowledge captures which subtask should be executed and when it is complete, while Action Knowledge captures object-relative geometric strategies and their physical effects. During execution, the agent retrieves knowledge at the corresponding decision level and grounds it in the current scene under the task goal. Across episodes, physical feedback is used to revise historical knowledge, update its applicability, and organize reusable entries for subsequent retrieval. Experiments on RMBench show that HPK improves average success by up to 24.2 percentage points across different agent models. With 80 interaction rollouts, held-out success rises from 48.3% to 75.0% for GPT-5.5 and from 70.0% to 88.3% for GPT-6. RoboHarn-Evo also resolves over 83% of historical knowledge errors while retaining 95.8% of valid knowledge, and transfers zero-shot from RMBench to RoboDojo with gains of 35.0 and 25.0 percentage points. These results demonstrate that physical interaction can be accumulated into reusable knowledge for improving subsequent manipulation.

</details>


### [86] [Credit-Guided Policy Improvement for Test-time Adaptive Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37591)
*Yang Li,Sijia Zhang,Yihan Li,Aming WU,Zihao Zhang,Ziju Han,Yahong Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Credit-Guided Policy Improvement (CGPI)，通过动作引发的观测转移恢复带符号、相对参考的决策信用，从而在测试时仅凭交互历史对冻结的导航策略进行可靠的轻量自适应更新，并在多个 VLN 基准上取得稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时自适应（TTA-VLN）在分布偏移下会扭曲局部动作偏好，导致偏离航线的决策。现有方法依赖预测不确定性、轨迹级反馈或累积自适应经验来纠偏，但这些信号无法直接揭示某个已执行动作是否真正支持指令引导的朝目标进展；而且即使有看似合理的纠偏信号，也不能保证策略更新是可靠的。因此核心挑战有两个：识别能支持目标导向改进的交互，以及判断由此产生的更新是否值得保留。

Method: 作者观察到每个已执行动作都会引发即时的观测转移，这为其局部后果提供了证据。基于这一洞见，提出 CGPI：在无外部结果反馈的情况下，从动作引发的观测转移中恢复带符号的、相对参考的决策信用。在预训练导航策略保持冻结的前提下，CGPI 利用该信用提出轻量的自适应更新，并将其与先前信用支持的交互进行校验：仅当更新获得支持时才保留，否则回滚。

Result: CGPI 在所评估的 VLN 基准和多种导航骨干网络上均取得一致的性能提升；定性机器人试验进一步展示了零样本仿真到现实迁移的可行性。

Conclusion: 从动作引发的观测转移中提取决策信用，并进行支持性校验与回滚，能够为 TTA-VLN 提供可靠且有效的策略改进，并具备向真实机器人迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation for vision-language navigation (TTA-VLN) enables pretrained policies to adapt online to unseen environments using only test-time observations and interaction history. However, distribution shifts can distort local action preferences and lead to off-course decisions. Existing methods rely on predictive uncertainty, trajectory-level feedback, or accumulated adaptation experience to correct such deviations. These signals, however, do not directly reveal whether an executed action supports instruction-guided progress toward the goal. Moreover, a plausible corrective signal does not guarantee a reliable policy update. The key challenge is thus twofold: identifying interactions that support goal-directed improvement and determining whether the resulting updates are worth retaining. We observe that each executed action induces an immediate observation transition, providing evidence of its local consequences. Based on this insight, we propose Credit-Guided Policy Improvement (CGPI), which recovers signed, reference-relative decision credit from action-induced observation transitions without external outcome feedback. With the pretrained navigation policy frozen, CGPI uses this credit to propose lightweight adaptation updates and verifies them against prior credit-supported interactions. Updates are retained only when supported and rolled back otherwise. CGPI achieves consistent gains across the evaluated VLN benchmarks and navigation backbones, while qualitative robot trials further illustrate the feasibility of zero-shot sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [87] [BlenDAgger: Blended Shared Control for Interactive Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37599)
*Cailyn Smith,Geoffrey Sun,Henny Admoni,Zackory Erickson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 BlenDAgger，通过在干预期间混合策略与人类动作（共享控制）来采集示教数据，提升模仿学习策略的自主性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略常依赖人类纠正数据训练，但遥操作纠正负担重，且人类示教者并非总是最优。

Method: BlenDAgger 使用共享控制在干预时将策略动作与示教者动作进行混合，从而采集用于训练模仿学习策略的数据。

Result: 在五项操作任务（2个真实、3个仿真）上验证：两个真实任务中自主性能比 HG-DAgger 提升30个百分点以上；控制切换更平滑（+57%），轨迹与训练数据相似度更高（+14%）；用户研究（n=14）显示数据采集更快（BF=13.32），主观感受无差异。

Conclusion: 相比完全遥操作干预的微调方法，混合共享控制能带来更高的自主性能。

Abstract: Robot policies are frequently trained from human corrections, yet teleoperating a robot to provide corrections is burdensome, and human demonstrators are not always optimal. We propose Blended DAgger (BlenDAgger), an approach for collecting data to train imitation learning policies by using shared control to blend the policy's and demonstrator's actions during interventions. By blending human and policy actions, we aim to improve the autonomous performance of manipulation policies. We validate our approach across five manipulation tasks, two in the real world and three in simulation. Our approach achieves higher autonomous performance by 30 or more percentage points on two real-world tasks compared to a typical human-gated correction approach (HG-DAgger). We also investigate the advantages of BlenDAgger that allow for higher autonomous performance, finding that BlenDAgger results in 57% smoother transitions between policy control and human interventions, and 14% higher trajectory similarity to the training data. In a user study (n=14) on two real-world tasks, we find that BlenDAgger results in faster data collection (BF=13.32), and we do not find a difference in subjective perceptions. These results show that blended shared control leads to higher autonomous performance compared to typical methods for fine-tuning robot policies from fully teleoperated interventions.

</details>


### [88] [When to Adapt: Multi-Signal Domain Shift Detection for Efficient Training-Free Adaptation in Open-Vocabulary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37602)
*Michele Antonazzi,Alejandra C. Hernandez,José Araujo,Olov Andersson,Patric Jensfelt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多信号域偏移检测方法，用于开放词汇分割中的免训练持续测试时自适应（TF-CTTA），通过监测视觉变化、适配器不匹配和语义漂移，仅在需要时触发自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境下长期运行的自主机器人需要稳健可靠的感知。视觉基础模型虽提升了开放词汇语义分割，但仍面临域偏移问题。现有免训练域自适应方法逐帧执行自适应，对资源受限的机器人硬件不切实际。

Method: 利用连续帧间的时间一致性，监测并融合域偏移的互补信号（视觉变化、适配器不匹配、语义漂移），构建多信号域偏移检测方法，仅在检测到偏移时触发自适应，用于开放词汇分割的免训练持续测试时自适应。

Result: 在包含室内和室外环境的基准上，并使用真实机器人数据验证。结果表明方法在保持分割精度的同时大幅减少自适应次数。

Conclusion: 该方法使免训练自适应在长期、真实世界的机器人部署中变得实用可行。

Abstract: Robust and reliable perception is essential for autonomous robots operating in real-world environments, particularly in long-term missions where environmental conditions may change significantly over time. Although recent advances in Visual Foundation Models (VFMs) have improved open-vocabulary semantic segmentation, these models can still suffer from domain shift, which can significantly degrade performance if they are not adapted to the current environment. Training-free domain adaptation is a relevant paradigm for adaptation, consisting of adjusting the model online using lightweight adapters. Recent approaches apply this on a per-frame basis, which is impractical for deployments on resource-constrained robotic hardware. To tackle this, we propose a multi-signal domain shift detection method for training-free continual test-time adaptation (TF-CTTA) in open-vocabulary segmentation. Our method leverages temporal coherence across consecutive frames by monitoring and combining complementary aspects of domain shift (visual change, adapter mismatch, and semantic drift) to trigger adaptation only when needed. We validate our approach on a benchmark including indoor and outdoor environments and using real robotic data. We demonstrate that our approach maintains segmentation accuracy while substantially reducing adaptations, making training-free adaptation practical and feasible for long-term, real-world robotic deployments.

</details>


### [89] [Cascaded consensus splitting for multi-branch contingency games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37612)
*Bastien Lechardoy,Pau de las Heras Molins,Thibault Lahire,Laurent Pautet,David Filliat,David Fridovich-Keil,Georgios Bakirtzis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出多分支偶然性架构与ADMM求解器，解决现有偶然性博弈中单一分支时间与计算成本高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偶然性博弈用单一分支时间处理意图不确定性，忽略了不同智能体意图在不同时刻揭示的异步性，且随智能体与意图数量增加，计算成本迅速增长。

Method: 提出多分支偶然性架构，允许意图不确定性在不同分支时间逐步解决，使轨迹适配意图的异步揭示；并开发基于ADMM的求解器以利用场景级并行性。

Result: 在紧密耦合的三智能体交互实验中，所提架构优于传统单分支方法，同时实现了更低的滚动时域平均求解时间。

Conclusion: 多分支架构能更好刻画异步意图揭示并提升计算效率，适用于多智能体交互规划。

Abstract: Contingency games enable agents to anticipate and plan for other agents' hypothetical intents by constructing trajectories with a shared prefix and intent-dependent branches. While contingency games capture intent uncertainty, existing formulations rely on a single branching time, oversimplifying interactions in which different agents' intentions are revealed at different times. Moreover, the computational cost of such problems grows rapidly with the number of agents and intents, as all scenario-dependent best responses must be solved jointly. We introduce a multi-branch contingency architecture in which sources of intent uncertainty can be resolved progressively at different branching times, allowing the planned trajectories to adapt to the asynchronous revelation of intents. We also develop an ADMM-based solver that exploits scenario-level parallelism. Experiments on tightly coupled three-agent interactions support that the proposed architecture outperforms the conventional single-branch while achieving a lower mean receding-horizon solve time.

</details>


### [90] [Semantic Map Sharing and Capability-Aware Coverage Planning for AI-Native 6G Robotic Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37666)
*Abdulqader Dhafer,Qi Wang,Zhou Daniel Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出边缘中心、语义感知的覆盖规划框架，融合空中地形感知、机器人可达性推理与高效语义共享，服务空地异构搜救团队。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构空地机器人搜救中，传统覆盖方法未将感知地形转化为平台特定可达性，且持续图像交换通信开销大。

Method: 将空中观测转为紧凑语义栅格图，进行可达性约束区域分解与能力感知覆盖路径规划，仅分配各机器人能力允许区域；形成感知-共享-规划闭环，用语义修正更新可达性推理与重规划。

Result: 高更新场景下语义修正传输使应用载荷较周期全图共享降低约82倍；基准场景中覆盖率达91.5%且无能力不可行分配，对比LS-MCPP为78.8%覆盖率和21.5%能力不可行分配率。

Conclusion: 语义修正可在无需反复传输完整地图的情况下更新共享规划状态，提升覆盖效率并降低通信成本。

Abstract: Search and Rescue (SAR) operations increasingly deploy heterogeneous teams of aerial and ground robots. However, conventional coverage methods typically do not translate perceived terrain into platform-specific reachability, while continuous image exchange imposes a high communication cost. We propose an edge-centric, semantic-aware coverage planning framework that integrates aerial terrain perception, robot-specific traversability reasoning, and payload-efficient semantic state sharing. Aerial observations are converted into compact semantic grid maps, enabling reachability-constrained area decomposition and capability-aware coverage paths that assign only regions admitted by each robot's capability profile. The resulting perception-sharing-planning loop feeds semantic corrections into traversability reasoning and replanning, forming an application-level mechanism motivated by AI-enabled goal-oriented communication envisioned for AI-native 6G networks. For the high-update case, transmitting semantic corrections reduces the application payload by a factor of approximately $82$ relative to periodic full-map sharing. Across matched benchmark scenarios, the proposed method achieved $91.5\%$ coverage with no capability-infeasible allocations, compared with $78.8\%$ coverage and a $21.5\%$ capability-infeasible allocation rate for LS-MCPP. Semantic corrections update the shared planning state without requiring repeated transmission of the complete map.

</details>


### [91] [Learning Expressive and Compositional Motion Representation via Spectral Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37677)
*Feiyang Wu,Chenxiao Gao,Chen Yang,Ye Zhao,Bo Dai,Anqi Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"spectral skills"——一种用于人形机器人分层控制中规划器-控制器接口的潜在表示，通过预测性表示学习获得，实现了精准跟踪、无需过渡策略的技能串联以及新行为的正交组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人通用控制常采用分层架构（规划器+控制器），其效果高度依赖二者之间的命令接口。该接口需同时满足三个要求：支持精准执行、易于预测、并能由已有行为组合出新行为。现有接口难以兼顾这些要求。

Method: 提出spectral skills作为接口的潜在表示，通过预测性表示学习获得：紧凑编码短运动片段，且学习目标是预测后续运动而非重构编码器输入。在29自由度人形机器人上训练一个以spectral skills为条件的控制器。

Result: 相比当前最优方法，全局跟踪误差降低62%；同一冻结控制器可直接串联独立编码的技能而无需单独的过渡策略；通过在任意兼容基础技能上叠加正交方向，可组合出训练数据中未出现的新行为。在Unitree G1硬件上验证了跟踪、串联、组合以及语言条件规划器控制。

Conclusion: spectral skills为分层人形控制提供了一种兼具精准执行、可预测性和可组合性的命令接口，并在真实硬件上得到验证。

Abstract: Robotic foundation models offer a promising path toward general-purpose humanoid robot control, often through hierarchical architectures. However, their effectiveness depends on the command interface between the planner and the controller, which must support accurate execution while remaining easy to predict, and ideally allow new behaviors to be composed from prior ones. In this work, we introduce spectral skills, a latent representation of this interface that meets these requirements through predictive representation learning. By design, spectral skills compactly encode short motion segments and are learned by predicting subsequent motion rather than reconstructing the encoder input. On a 29-DoF humanoid, a controller conditioned on spectral skills reduces global tracking error by 62\% relative to the state of the art. The same frozen controller chains independently encoded skills without a separate transition policy. It also composes new behaviors by adding orthogonal directions to any compatible base skill, producing combinations unseen in the training data. We demonstrate tracking, chaining, and composition, as well as control through a language-conditioned planner, on Unitree G1 hardware. Project page: https://spectral-skill.github.io

</details>


### [92] [ProAct-VLM: Pre-Failure Vision-Language Task Replanning with Continuous Perception Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37681)
*Ahmed Nader Ahmed,Omar Moured,Mughni Irfan Mohammed Abdul,Muhayy Ud Din,Irfan Hussain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ProAct-VLM，一种将 VLM 嵌入实时感知-反馈闭环的自适应物理任务规划框架，在环境变化检测到时立即重规划，从而在执行失败前完成适应，提升动态长时域操作任务的成功率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域机器人任务易受环境突发变化影响，导致既定动作失效或不安全。传统基于规则的决策流程在开放世界中脆弱，缺乏泛化能力。VLM 具备世界知识与图文推理能力，可生成接地气的任务规划，但必须嵌入能处理不确定性与环境变化的框架中。现有框架多为反应式（失败后或任务后检查才重规划），或仅在动作前做离散条件校验，会遗漏执行过程中的变化。

Method: ProAct-VLM 是一个自适应、物理接地的任务规划框架：将 VLM 集成到实时感知-反馈闭环中，持续监控环境，一旦检测到相关变化便立即重新规划，从而在执行失败发生之前实现自适应。框架强调持续监控与即时重规划的机制，而非离散的失败后触发。

Result: 在多个基线和不同 VLM 主干上的评估表明，该框架在动态、长时域操作任务中同时提升了成功率与执行效率（项目页面：https://github.com/moured/ProAct-VLM）。

Conclusion: 通过将 VLM 置于实时感知-反馈闭环内实现持续监控与即时重规划，ProAct-VLM 有效克服了现有反应式框架的滞后问题，为动态真实环境中的长时域机器人规划提供了更鲁棒、更高效的方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic tasks are vulnerable to unexpected environmental changes that can render planned actions ineffective or unsafe. To address this, robots must detect such changes as they occur, interpret their impact, and adjust their actions accordingly. Traditional rule-based decision-making pipelines are brittle in open-world conditions, as they are hand-tuned for specific scenarios and lack generalization. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer a promising alternative as they combine broad world knowledge with unified visual--text reasoning, enabling them to generalize across diverse scenarios and generate accurate, grounded task plans. However, for effective deployment in dynamic real-world settings, VLMs must be embedded into frameworks capable of handling uncertainty and environmental changes. Existing frameworks broadly address this reactively, triggering replanning only after execution failures or post-task checks, risking failed actions. Some methods verify conditions before actions, but these discrete checks miss changes occurring during execution. To address this, we present ProAct-VLM, an adaptive, physically grounded task planning framework that integrates VLMs within a real-time perception--feedback loop. ProAct-VLM continuously monitors the environment and re-plans as soon as relevant changes are detected, enabling adaptation before failure occurs. Evaluations against multiple baselines and across different VLM backbones show that our framework improves both success rates and efficiency in dynamic, long-horizon manipulation tasks. Project page: https://github.com/moured/ProAct-VLM

</details>


### [93] [CogWAM: Aligning Semantic Cognition with World Action Modeling via Event-Driven Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37721)
*Sen Wang,Liu Liu,Xinjiang Wang,Zequn Chen,Haoyi Jiang,Taojun Ding,Tingyang Xiao,Zhizhong Su,Jie Wang,Sanping Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CogWAM是一种认知引导的世界-动作模型，通过持久语义状态建立任务推理与世界-动作学习之间的显式语义接口，实现任务进度对齐的机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略虽融合语义推理与未来预测，但两者结合并不能保证局部预测与动作始终与任务进度保持一致，缺乏统一的进度上下文。

Method: 引入持久化的Semantic State存储已完成任务事件与当前子任务，仅在观测出现语义转换时更新；采用progress-conditioned的WORLD与ACTION查询选择性提取任务相关信息；训练时语义状态为两分支提供共享任务进度上下文，推理时移除预测分支，直接从观测与维护状态生成动作；并提出语义训练策略改进转换学习与闭环条件化。

Result: 无需额外机器人动作预训练，在RoboDojo上达到15.56/11.70% Score/SR，在BiCoord上取得最优性能；真实世界实验实现闭环双臂操作，语义状态重生成次数比逐步更新减少16.4次。

Conclusion: 通过显式语义接口与持久状态，CogWAM有效对齐任务进度与世界-动作学习，在仿真与真实环境中均验证了其高效性与优越性能。

Abstract: Robot policies increasingly incorporate semantic reasoning and future-world prediction, yet combining these capabilities does not guarantee that local predictions and actions remain aligned with task progress. We introduce CogWAM, a cognition-guided world-action model that establishes an explicit semantic interface between task reasoning and world-action learning through a persistent Semantic State, which stores completed task events and the active subtask. CogWAM updates this state only when observations indicate semantic transitions, allowing task-level context to persist across multiple action chunks. To bridge semantic context with physical prediction and control, CogWAM employs progress-conditioned WORLD and ACTION queries that selectively extract task-relevant information for future-world prediction and action generation. During training, the Semantic State provides shared task-progress context for both branches, while inference removes the future-prediction branch and directly generates actions from observations and the maintained state. We further introduce semantic training strategies to improve transition learning and closed-loop conditioning. Without additional robot-action pretraining, CogWAM achieves 15.56 / 11.70 % Score/SR on RoboDojo and state-of-the-art performance on BiCoord, while real-world experiments demonstrate closed-loop dual-arm manipulation with 16.4 fewer Semantic State regenerations than step-wise updating.

</details>


### [94] [Recompositional Robotics: Cross-Domain, Open-set, and Lifelong Modularity Beyond Morphology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37734)
*Steven Swanbeck,Jonathan Salfity,Corrie Van Sice,Robert Blake Anderson,Mitch Pryor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"重组机器人学"（recompositional robotics）这一新概念：不追求结构可变，而是让机器人在动力学、感知、计算与高层行为等多域上可重组，以"整合跨度"和"整合惯性"为核心度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模块化机器人研究主要聚焦于结构域的可重构性（形态在线改变），但在感知、计算、协调行为等其他决定机器人根本能力的域上，重组阻力依然很高。作者希望从"结构可变"转向"能力可变"，即让跨域重组成为可能，从而获得更大的适应性与实用价值。

Method: 作者以概念化与问题定义为主：提出"整合跨度"（integration span，指跨多个域可重组的范围）与"整合惯性"（integration inertia，指对重组的抵抗程度）两个互补度量；主张最优系统应最大化跨度、最小化惯性；并将硬件、软件、计算与行为统一抽象为模块的接口（所需接口与所提供接口），以便整体化推理。文中将问题锚定于两个实际部署系统与若干在研工作，并据此展开开放性问题讨论。

Result: 本文为立场/概念性论文，未提供实验量化结果。主要产出是将"重组机器人学"形式化为一类适应性问题，给出跨度与惯性两个度量维度，并指出当前模块化机器人在结构域惯性低、在其他域惯性高这一现状，同时提出若干未来研究方向与开放问题。

Conclusion: 最大化整合跨度并最小化整合惯性是让重组在实践中最具效用的关键，为此需要将硬件、软件、计算与行为以统一接口抽象并整体推理，这构成了"重组机器人学"的核心挑战。作者界定了该问题并呼吁后续研究回答其开放性问题。

Abstract: Research in modular robotics has produced capable approaches allowing a robot's morphology to change online, with recent efforts also developing approaches to decide which morphology to assume and automatically propagate that decision into the robot's motion planning and control. These approaches are powerful and increase adaptability in the field. However, an alternative objective is not to build robots whose structures can change, but robots whose fundamental capabilities can change, where capability is a joint function across several domains, including kino-dynamics, perception, compute, and high-level coordinating behaviors. A robot designed to be reconfigured across these domains has a greater capacity to alter its capability than one that can be reconfigured in a single domain. We refer to this cross-domain reconfigurability as integration span, and recognize a complementary measure of the resistance to reconfiguration, which we refer to as integration inertia. Current modular robots have reduced integration inertia in the structural domain while it remains high in the other domains that contribute to integration span. We assert that the systems that can provide the most utility through reconfiguration in practice are those maximizing span and minimizing inertia and call this general problem recompositional robotics: adaptation over a heterogeneous set of modules including hardware, software, compute, and behavior that abstracts each component by the interfaces it requires and provides such that they can be reasoned over holistically. We define the problem, ground it in two deployed systems and active research efforts, and pose open questions about the future of recompositional robotics.

</details>


### [95] [Faster and Better? Benchmark Bugs and Design Limitations Distort the Evaluation of Vision-Language-Action Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37771)
*Qiwei Chen,Kaijun Zhou,Nuohui Shi,Zhiyang Li,Yuxuan Feng,Jinyu Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文发现VLA加速方法在仿真基准上异常超越基线，通过审计定位出22个bug和4类设计缺陷，修复后可逆转方法排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真操作基准是评估VLA策略及其免训练加速方法的标准工具，但作者观察到部分加速方法测得成功率竟高于基线本身。仅凭成功率无法判断这些增益来自更好的任务执行还是评测缺陷，因此需要系统审计基准的可靠性。

Method: 从出现异常增益的任务出发，通过将物体轨迹与检查器接受区域对比来定位根因，把bug分为任务一致性、初始化与可复现性三类；将审计扩展到RoboTwin、LIBERO-Plus、VLABench等七个基准，并针对设计缺陷修正宽松的成功检查器、改正不真实的物体质量、加入偏好平滑动作的运动感知评分。

Result: 共识别出22个bug与4类设计缺陷。修复bug可逆转方法排名，使基线在某任务上从最后升至第一；解决设计缺陷同样能消除异常增益，某任务上基线从落后加速方法21个百分点变为领先5个百分点。

Conclusion: 归因于加速的性能增益可能是基准伪影而非真实的任务执行提升；作者公开bug修复与修订后的基准设置，以支持VLA加速的可信评估。

Abstract: Simulated manipulation benchmarks are the standard tool for evaluating vision-language-action (VLA) policies and the acceleration methods that reduce their inference latency for on-robot deployment. On these benchmarks, we observe that some training-free acceleration methods, which approximate the baseline policy's computation, achieve higher measured success rates than the baseline itself. Success rates alone cannot establish whether such gains come from better task execution or from evaluation flaws. We therefore investigate two kinds of benchmark flaws behind these gains: bugs, where the implementation does not match the intended task or evaluation protocol, and design limitations, where success criteria and simulation settings do not fully capture how acceleration affects task execution. Starting from tasks with anomalous gains, we localize root causes by plotting object trajectories against checker acceptance regions, and classify the resulting bugs into task consistency, initialization, and reproducibility. Extending this audit to seven benchmarks, including RoboTwin, LIBERO-Plus, and VLABench, we identify 22 bugs of these types and 4 design limitations. For the latter, we revise permissive success checkers, correct unrealistic object masses, and add a motion-aware score that favors smoother actions. Experiments show that bug fixes can reverse method rankings, moving the baseline from last to first on one task. Addressing design limitations can likewise remove anomalous gains: on another task, the baseline moves from 21 percentage points behind an accelerated method to 5 points ahead. Gains attributed to acceleration can therefore be artifacts of the benchmark rather than better task execution. We release our bug fixes and revised benchmark settings to support trustworthy evaluation of VLA acceleration.

</details>


### [96] [Urgent Actions Go First: Urgency-Aware Denoising for Real-Time VLA Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37772)
*Zibo Wang,Haochen Han,Pengzhen Ren,Mingtong Dai,Fangming Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Urgency-Aware Denoising (UAD)推理框架，利用动作块的异构执行紧迫性分配去噪计算，实现最高1.89倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion与flow-matching VLA策略通过迭代去噪生成动作块，推理延迟高，严重限制实时机器人控制。现有加速方法将动作块视为单一整体计算单元，忽略了滚动时域控制中的物理现实：动作虽联合生成却顺序执行，因此具有内在异构的执行紧迫性。

Method: UAD根据每个动作实际被需要的时机分配去噪计算：时间紧迫的紧急动作在较少去噪步数后即被释放，尾部动作的持续后台精化与物理执行重叠。针对异构去噪带来的两个挑战——紧急动作的提前释放误差与尾部动作的轨迹不一致——提出两个核心机制：Trajectory Reconciliation重构统一的内部状态演化，在不增加额外模型评估的情况下恢复联合去噪一致性；Ghost Action Correction利用未执行的ghost延续动态补偿剩余可执行动作上的提前释放误差。

Result: 在多种VLA架构、仿真基准和真实世界操作任务上进行的大量评估表明，UAD在保持与最优去噪预算下vanilla推理相当成功率的同时，平均动作可用延迟最高加速1.89倍，相比最先进的VLA加速基线提供更优的成功率-延迟权衡。

Conclusion: UAD通过挖掘动作块执行紧迫性的异构性，在推理阶段实现自适应去噪计算分配，显著降低动作可用延迟而不牺牲任务成功率，为实时VLA机器人控制提供了更优的加速方案。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-matching Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies generate action chunks through iterative denoising, incurring substantial inference latency that severely limits real-time robotic control. Existing acceleration methods treat an action chunk as a monolithic computational unit, ignoring a crucial physical reality of receding-horizon control: actions are generated jointly but consumed sequentially, resulting in inherently heterogeneous execution urgencies. We exploit this asymmetry to introduce Urgency-Aware Denoising (UAD), a novel inference-time framework that allocates denoising computation according to when each action is physically needed. UAD releases time-critical urgent actions after fewer denoising steps while overlapping the continued background refinement of tail actions with physical execution. However, heterogeneous denoising introduces two key challenges: early-release errors in urgent actions and trajectory inconsistency in tail actions. UAD elegantly resolves both through two core mechanisms: Trajectory Reconciliation, which reconstructs unified internal state evolution to restore joint denoising coherence without additional model evaluations, and Ghost Action Correction, which leverages non-executed ghost continuations to dynamically compensate for early-release errors across remaining executable actions. Extensive evaluations across multiple VLA architectures, simulation benchmarks, and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that UAD achieves up to a 1.89x speedup in average action availability latency while maintaining comparable success rates to vanilla inference with optimal denoising budget, offering a more favorable success-latency trade-off than state-of-the-art VLA acceleration baselines.

</details>


### [97] [Geometry-Preserving Human-to-Robot Upper-Body Motion Retargeting from Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37776)
*Xiaoyu Yang,Sen Han,Da Li,Nan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何保真的人到机器人运动重定向框架，将统一的身体-手部重建与形态无关的几何迁移结合，实现单目视频到双臂灵巧机器人的协同上半身运动迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目RGB视频是获取人类示教的便捷来源，但视频驱动的人到机器人迁移面临三大难题：身体与手部运动在不同空间尺度上恢复、人类与机器人运动学差异显著、以及复杂远端（手部）运动难以跨形态保留。

Method: 将逐帧身体估计、视频级观测与手部细节证据共同约束到一个可微的Momentum Human Rig (MHR)状态中，并在参数空间修复手部瞬态伪影；用臂段方向、肘部构型、相对手掌朝向及双侧腕关系表示运动，再通过多阶段逆运动学与机器人专属手部适配在目标机器人上实现。系统中Across-VAM负责视频生成，Across-WAM负责人到机器人运动映射。

Result: 在16段单目视频（共1,769帧，含10段手势与6段抓取任务）上评估。统一重建将平均手部重投影误差从SAM 3D Body的22.36像素降至7.21像素；16条重定向轨迹全部完成运动学仿真回放，代表性手势与抓取动作并在实体机器人上演示。

Conclusion: 结果证明了一条从单目人类视频到双臂灵巧机器人协同上半身运动的统一流水线是可行的。

Abstract: Monocular RGB video provides an accessible source of human demonstrations for upper-body robot motion, yet video-driven human-to-robot transfer remains challenging because body and hand motion are recovered at different spatial scales, human and robot kinematics differ substantially, and fine distal motion is difficult to preserve across embodiments. We present a geometry-preserving motion-retargeting framework that integrates unified body--hand reconstruction with morphology-independent geometric transfer. Frame-wise body estimates, video-level observations, and detailed hand evidence jointly constrain a single differentiable Momentum Human Rig (MHR) state, while transient hand artifacts are repaired in parameter space. The reconstructed motion is represented by arm-segment directions, elbow configuration, relative palm orientation, and bilateral wrist relations, and is realized on the target robot through multi-stage inverse kinematics and robot-specific hand adaptation. Within the broader system, Across-VAM provides video generation, whereas Across-WAM performs human-to-robot motion mapping. The method is evaluated on 16 monocular videos comprising 1,769 source frames, including 10 signing and six reach-to-grasp sequences. Unified reconstruction reduces mean hand reprojection error from 22.36 to 7.21 pixels relative to SAM 3D Body. All 16 retargeted trajectories completed kinematic simulation playback, and representative signing and reach-to-grasp motions were further demonstrated on a physical robot. The results demonstrate a unified pipeline from monocular human video to coordinated upper-body motion on a dual-arm dexterous robot.

</details>


### [98] [MVG-WAM: Multiple View Geometry-Aware World-Action Modeling for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37793)
*Wenbo Chen,Tianfu Li,Haoxuan Xu,Zhihao Cao,Zhenghan Chen,Zhengming Zhu,Zizhou Luo,Guosheng Yang,Yuan Liu,Lujia Wang,Wen Chen,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MVG-WAM，将多相机观测建模为同一物理世界的相关投影而非拼贴图像，显式引入几何关系，实现高成功率的多视图机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有World-Action Models的多视图接口通常只是拼接图像或串联token，导致同步相机间的几何关系被隐式处理，难以将全局场景上下文与交互所需的局部几何联系起来。

Method: MVG-WAM结合了受对极约束的全局状态与视图索引的几何状态（由同步观测联合推断）；采用相机感知路由为每个视频区域提供对应的几何上下文和共享全局状态；并通过多时域未来深度监督将几何感知表示锚定在度量尺度上，且动作推演时无需深度解码。

Result: 在LIBERO上平均成功率99.1%，RoboTwin 2.0上92.07%；在Cobot Magic真实实验中，跨三项操作任务的150次试验成功率为91.3%。

Conclusion: 显式建模多视图几何关系能有效提升世界-动作模型的表示质量与操作性能，兼具仿真与真实场景的竞争力。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) couple visual dynamics with action prediction, bringing the rich priors of pretrained video models to robotic manipulation. However, their multi-view interfaces typically tile images or concatenate tokens, leaving the geometric relationships among synchronized cameras implicit. This makes it harder to connect global scene context with the local geometry required for interaction. We introduce the Multi-View Geometry-Aware World-Action Model (MVG-WAM), which organizes these observations as related projections of one physical world rather than separate images on a canvas. Our model combines an epipolar-constrained global state with view-indexed geometric states jointly inferred from synchronized observations. Camera-aware routing supplies each video region with its corresponding geometric context and the shared global state, explicitly structuring the representation used for action prediction. We further ground the geometry-aware representation in metric scale through multi-horizon future-depth supervision, without requiring depth decoding during action rollout. MVG-WAM achieves average success rates of 99.1% on LIBERO and 92.07% on RoboTwin 2.0, demonstrating competitive performance across both benchmarks. Real-world experiments on Cobot Magic further demonstrate a 91.3% success rate across 150 trials spanning three manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [99] [Explore, Execute, Evolve: A Skill Acquisition and Reuse Loop for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37810)
*Sicheng Xie,Yitong Chen,Haidong Cao,Shunlin Lu,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboSkill 通过“探索-执行-进化”闭环连接技能获取与复用，结合视觉-触觉反馈与可复用代码，显著提升零样本机器人任务成功率并降低执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态智能体虽能零样本完成机器人任务，但需从零推理和探索物理世界，执行成本高、泛化到未见任务困难。亟需降低这种探索与推理开销。

Method: 提出 RoboSkill 框架，构建 Explore–Execute–Evolve 闭环：智能体探索收集任务相关信息，执行任务时适应反馈，并基于执行记录进化技能库；下一轮复用技能引导探索与执行。同时用触觉反馈补充视觉以降低物理交互不确定性，并用可复用代码增强文本指导以减少技能复用时的推理开销。

Result: 在 LIBERO-10 上，四个智能体的首回合成功率提升 12.5–25.0 个百分点，平均运行时间降低 7.6–72.4%。在真实机器人上，成功率提升 8.3 个百分点，成功试验的平均运行时间至少降低 14.4%。

Conclusion: RoboSkill 通过技能获取与复用闭环及多模态增强，有效提升了机器人任务求解的效率与成功率，且适用于仿真与真实场景。

Abstract: Vision-language-action and world-action models have demonstrated impressive capabilities in robotics, yet generalization to unseen tasks remains challenging. More recently, general-purpose multimodal agents have shown great potential for zero-shot robotic task solving. However, they often incur high execution costs by reasoning and exploring the physical world from scratch. To reduce these costs, we introduce RoboSkill, a framework that connects skill acquisition and reuse through an Explore, Execute, Evolve loop. Within this loop, the agent explores to gather task-relevant information, executes tasks while adapting to feedback, and evolves its skill library based on execution records. It then reuses these skills to guide exploration and execution in the next cycle, closing the loop. To improve loop efficiency, we complement vision with tactile feedback to reduce uncertainty during physical interaction. We further augment textual guidance with reusable code to reduce reasoning overhead during skill reuse. On LIBERO-10, RoboSkill improves first-episode success rates by 12.5--25.0 percentage points and reduces average runtime by 7.6--72.4% across four agents. On real robots, it improves success rates by 8.3 percentage points and reduces average runtime for successful trials by at least 14.4%.

</details>


### [100] [ExceptionDrive: A Planning-Oriented Counterfactual Corner-Case Benchmark for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37871)
*Ziyi Luo,Zhe Sun,Yehao Lu,Lei Zhou,Lisheng Wu,Xuewei Li,Zequn Qin,Xi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ExceptionDrive，一个通过 VLM 辅助的隐患插入与无参考评估协议来检验规划器安全可靠性的反事实规划基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规驾驶基准的平均性能无法证明规划器在罕见、安全攸关危险下的可靠性，缺乏针对此类长尾风险的系统评测手段。

Method: 用 VLM 辅助筛选、局部多视图编辑与质量审计，在真实 nuScenes 场景中插入隐患并保留上下文；构建覆盖六个安全族、21 项任务的基准，定义危险/冲突区域、局部安全约束与可接受响应；提出无参考评估协议，指标含 UR、HCC、HPR、CTS；并设计 Reminder Agent，将视觉证据与共享分类体系转化为危险存在性、类型及高层策略建议的结构化记录，供 VLM 决策代理使用。

Result: 七种代表性规划器频繁侵入危险区域或给出不足的安全间距；零样本实验中，Reminder 记录提升了策略准确率并减少了预警不足（under-warning）。

Conclusion: ExceptionDrive 提供了可审计的危险注入基准与无参考安全评估协议，揭示现有规划器的长尾安全短板，并表明结构化的危险提醒能有效改善零样本决策安全表现。

Abstract: Average performance on routine driving benchmarks does not establish planner reliability under rare, safety-critical hazards. We proposed ExceptionDrive, a counterfactual planning benchmark that uses VLM-assisted screening, localized multi-view editing, and quality auditing to insert hazards into real nuScenes scenes while preserving their context. Its 21 tasks span six safety families and define hazard or conflict regions, local safety constraints, and acceptable responses. Because hazard insertion can invalidate the recorded human trajectory, our reference-free protocol evaluates edited predictions using Unsafe Rate (UR), Hazard Clearance Compliance (HCC), Hazard Proximity Response (HPR), and Counterfactual Trajectory Shift (CTS), which measure core-region intrusion, clearance compliance, clearance relative to a prescribed margin, and counterfactual trajectory change. Seven representative planners frequently intrude into hazard regions or provide insufficient clearance. We also develop a Reminder Agent that, without sample-specific task labels, converts visual evidence and the shared taxonomy into structured records of hazard presence, type, and a recommended high-level strategy. The agent neither predicts trajectories nor controls the vehicle; its records guide a VLM-based decision agent. In zero-shot experiments, the reminders improve strategy accuracy and reduce under-warning.

</details>


### [101] [WayFinder: Hierarchical Visual-Language-Action for Zero-Shot Waypoint Generation and Low-Level Kinematic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37922)
*Timothy K Johnsen,Marco Levorato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WayFinder是一种端到端闭环分层VLA框架，通过将高层任务推理与低层运动控制解耦，避免微调，在AirSim中实现更可靠的导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽具有强大泛化能力，但实际部署受限于执行不可靠以及针对特定机器人形态和任务进行微调的高昂计算成本。

Method: 采用分层闭环结构：利用零样本、离线的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）策略处理语言上下文与状态地图，生成战略性路标；同时由轻量级在机策略基于连续传感器反馈，异步执行高频实时运动控制。

Result: 在Microsoft AirSim中，针对四个复杂度不同的环境与三种MLLM规模进行评测。相比基线低层策略，WayFinder导航可靠性更优；仅在导航失败时调用高层MLLM，导航成功率最高提升27.45%。

Conclusion: WayFinder无需微调，减少了昂贵推理，显著提升导航成功率，为VLA模型的实用部署提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Visual Language Action (VLA) models offer unprecedented generalization for autonomous robots; however, their real-world deployment is frequently bottlenecked by unreliable execution and the prohibitive computational cost of fine-tuning for specific robot embodiments and tasks. To bridge this gap, we propose WayFinder, an end-to-end, closed-loop hierarchical VLA framework that circumvents the need for fine-tuning by decoupling high-level task reasoning from low-level kinematic control. WayFinder utilizes a zero-shot, offboard Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) policy to process linguistic context and state maps for strategic waypoint generation. Asynchronously, a lightweight, onboard policy executes real-time kinematic control at high frequency based on continuous sensor feedback. We evaluate WayFinder in Microsoft AirSim, testing on four environments of varying complexity and three MLLM scales to balance prediction efficacy with computational efficiency. Our results demonstrate that WayFinder achieves superior navigation reliability compared to baseline low-level policies. By querying the high-level MLLM only during navigation failures, WayFinder eliminates the need for fine-tuning, minimizes expensive inferences, and significantly increases navigation success rates by up to 27.45%.

</details>


### [102] [PhysWAM: Physically Consistent World Action Model for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37970)
*Dhruv Parikh,Fengcheng Yu,Quankai Gao,Jiawei Yang,Junjie Ye,Maulik Bhatt,Thang Vu,Charles Ochoa,Rowan McAllister,Igor Vasiljevic,Rajgopal Kannan,Viktor Prasanna,Vitor Guizilini,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysWAM 将多视角视频、度量深度与自车运动的联合去噪统一在单个 flow-matching transformer 中，并通过耦合点投影（CPP）约束生成几何与实测 LiDAR 一致，在 NAVSIM 规划与零样本闭环迁移上表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 world-action 模型虽联合预测场景演化与动作，但联合生成本身并不保证世界与动作预测共享统一的几何约束，缺乏与实测场景几何的一致性。

Method: 提出 PhysWAM：在单个 flow-matching transformer 内联合去噪多视角视频、度量深度与自车运动。引入耦合点投影（CPP），将生成深度反投影为 3D 点，使用生成的 SE(3) 自车运动变换后，最小化其与用记录自车运动变换的 LiDAR 点之间的距离，从而对深度与运动进行几何监督。推理阶段仅用无标签一致性规则选择轨迹，无需学习打分器或仿真器反馈。

Result: 在 NAVSIM v1/v2 规划、零样本闭环迁移及未来视频与度量深度预测上评估。尽管选择策略简单，仍取得较强规划性能，并可零样本迁移至未见驾驶环境；同时生成准确的度量深度与时间连贯视频，CPP 同时改善规划与深度预测。

Conclusion: 场景深度与自车运动之间的几何关系，为在简单统一模型中耦合世界生成与动作生成提供了直接途径。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) jointly predict how a scene will evolve and how an agent should act, however joint generation alone does not necessarily impose a shared geometric constraint on these predictions. We present PhysWAM, a unified world-action model for autonomous driving that co-denoises multiview video, metric depth, and ego motion within a single flow-matching transformer. To ground world and action generation in measured scene geometry, we introduce Coupled Point Projection (CPP) that unprojects the generated depth into 3D points, transforms them using the generated $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ ego motion, and minimizes their distance to LiDAR points transformed using the recorded ego motion. This geometric constraint promotes physical consistency with the measured scene by jointly supervising generated depth and motion alongside their standard flow-matching objectives. At inference, trajectory selection relies only on a simple label-free consensus rule, with no learned scorer or simulator feedback. We evaluate PhysWAM across NAVSIM v1 and v2 planning, zero-shot closed-loop transfer, and future video and metric-depth prediction. Despite PhysWAM's simple selection procedure, it achieves strong planning performance and transfers zero-shot to unseen driving environments. It also generates accurate metric depth and temporally coherent video, with CPP improving both planning and depth prediction. Together, these results demonstrate that the geometric relationship between scene depth and ego motion provides a direct way to couple world and action generation within a simple unified model.

</details>


### [103] [Comparing Utility of Inertial, Occupancy, Semantic, and Intent Information in Human Motion Prediction During Daily Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37971)
*Max Burns,Maisha Khanum,Monroe Kennedy,Steven H. Collins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究评估室内人体运动预测中不同信息源的重要性，使用扩散模型并结合Aria眼镜采集的室内日常活动数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统在有人环境中运行需要准确的室内人体运动预测，尤其在复杂室内空间。作者希望探究哪些信息源对预测最关键。

Method: 收集9名受试者佩戴Meta Aria眼镜进行模拟室内日常活动的数据集，涵盖10栋建筑、238分钟导航；采用人体运动扩散模型，比较基线（匀速）与加入身体运动、场景表示、眼动注视、语义信息、显式意图信息的效果。

Result: 相较匀速基线提升42%；加入身体运动、场景表示和眼动注视显著降低预测误差；语义信息对室内有用但低于户外导航；显式意图信息降误差效果最好；眼动注视对预测减速尤其有用，并在缺少占据地图时提供基础空间信息。

Conclusion: 显式意图估计或用户输入对更精细的室内运动预测至关重要；眼动注视是有用的意图指标。该工作为高度模糊的室内场景下人体运动预测迈出第一步。代码将在录用后公开。

Abstract: Accurate human motion prediction is crucial for robotic systems operating around people, particularly in complex indoor spaces. In this study, we assess the relative importance of different sources of information in indoor motion prediction with a human motion diffusion model. We collected a dataset of nine naive human subjects conducting simulated indoor daily activities while wearing a pair of Meta Aria glasses. This dataset includes ten buildings from a university campus, and encompasses 238 minutes of navigation between daily tasks. Overall, we demonstrate a 42% improvement beyond a constant velocity baseline. Including body motion, scene representation, and eye gaze fixation data significantly reduced prediction error. Semantic information was found to be useful for indoor motion prediction, but to a lesser degree than in outdoor navigation. Providing explicit intent information reduced error beyond any other addition, suggesting that incorporating explicit intent estimation or user input are fundamental for finer prediction of indoor motion. One of the few indicators of intent, eye gaze fixation, was found to be especially useful in predicting deceleration, and provided basic spatial information to the model in the absence of an occupancy map. These results are a first step towards predicting human motion in highly ambiguous indoor scenarios. Code will be made public upon acceptance. Project page: https://human-motion-diffusion.github.io/

</details>


### [104] [doPlan: A Variable-Horizon Dataset for Multi-Stage Language-Conditioned Planning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38028)
*Parthib Roy,Yash Tandon,Marcus Blennemann,Giovanni Tapia Lopez,Angel Martinez-Sanchez,Mohan M. Trivedi,Ross Greer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出doPlan——首个公开的、人工标注的真实世界数据集，用于研究乘客语言作为持续性任务上下文，并揭示现有语言条件驾驶模型难以将持续性乘客意图与后续规划决策联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言赋能的驾驶数据集多聚焦于短时、局部的交互，而乘客意图可能跨越多个行为阶段、依赖未来事件、指涉周围智能体或地标，并随驾驶条件演变而持续相关，这类长时程乘客意图仍未被充分探索。

Method: 基于nuPlan构建doPlan数据集，包含5,154条人工撰写的乘客指令，覆盖50.9小时独特驾驶中的169.1小时累计指令对齐上下文，标注窗口从30.0秒到508.8秒，涵盖即时、延迟、事件条件、持续和多阶段乘客意图；并对四种语言条件驾驶模型进行评估。

Result: 模型对乘客语言的敏感性并不能可靠转化为与请求方向一致的行为。在2,161个具有匹配未来机动动作的示例中，首个相关机动动作发生在评估点之后的中位数24.6秒，仅9.8%落在模型常见的5秒预测窗口内。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了将持续性乘客意图与连续规划决策相连接的必要性。doPlan为研究未解决目标如何被保留、如何在演变场景中落地、以及如何跨多阶段追踪（包括规划器何时判定未来目标对当前规划变得相关）提供了实验平台。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles interacting with passengers through natural language must reason beyond immediate commands. Passenger intent may span multiple stages of behavior, depend on future events, refer to surrounding agents or landmarks, and remain relevant as driving conditions evolve. Existing language-enabled driving datasets largely focus on short, localized interactions, leaving these longer-horizon forms of passenger intent comparatively underexplored. We introduce doPlan, to our knowledge the first publicly available, human-annotated real-world dataset designed to study passenger language as persistent task context. Built on nuPlan, doPlan contains 5,154 human-written passenger instructions spanning 169.1 hours of cumulative instruction-aligned context over 50.9 hours of unique driving, with annotation windows ranging from 30.0 to 508.8 s. The annotations capture immediate, deferred, event-conditioned, persistent, and multi-stage passenger intent. The dataset, annotation interface, and supporting resources are publicly available at https://github.com/Mi3-Lab/doPlan. We evaluate four language-conditioned driving models and find that sensitivity to passenger language does not reliably translate into behavior consistent with the requested direction. More broadly, among 2,161 examples with a matched future maneuver, the first associated maneuver occurs a median of 24.6 s after the evaluation point, and only 9.8% occur within the models' common 5 s prediction horizon. These findings highlight the need to connect persistent passenger intent with successive planning decisions. doPlan provides a setting for studying how unresolved goals can be retained, grounded in evolving scenes, and tracked across multiple stages, including how a planner determines when a future goal becomes relevant to the current plan.

</details>


### [105] [EgoAlign: Bridging the Human-Humanoid Gap for Long-Range Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38046)
*Yiming Jiang,Chen Jin,Chongyang Xu,Yilun Chen,Aimin Hao,Yisheng He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EgoAlign框架，将第一人称人类演示数据转化为适用于通用连续全身控制器的动作与状态监督，无需采集真机演示即可微调VLA模型并零样本部署到实体人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称人类演示是易得的任务经验来源，但存在人体尺度与控制响应差异、缺失机器人状态等问题，限制了它们作为人形机器人训练监督的价值。

Method: EgoAlign利用目标机器人模型与仿真器，通过执行反馈引导演示采集；保留视觉引导的周期性迈步运动参考，并通过尺度对齐与控制器在环细化来适配上半身交互几何；最后用因果回放重建对应的机器人状态与运动token标签，与人类观测共同用于训练。

Result: 仅用适配后的人类演示微调VLA模型并零样本部署到实体人形机器人，策略可完成长距离物体搬运、导航至未见目标位置及独立评估的足部交互；细化相比纯运动学对齐提升了仿真手部对齐与实物抓取成功率，人类采集相比遥操作减少了现场采集时间。

Conclusion: EgoAlign证明无需真机演示即可将第一人称人类演示转化为有效的人形机器人训练监督，实现零样本实体部署并提升数据采集效率。

Abstract: Egocentric human demonstrations offer an accessible source of task experience, but differences in body scale and controller response, together with missing robot states, limit their value as humanoid training supervision. We present EgoAlign, a data-construction framework that converts these demonstrations into action and state supervision compatible with a general-purpose, continuous whole-body controller, without collecting physical-robot demonstrations. Using the target-robot model and simulator, EgoAlign guides demonstration collection through execution feedback. It preserves locomotion references for visually guided periodic stepping while adapting upper-body interaction geometry through scale alignment and controller-in-the-loop refinement.
